### [1] [Analytical mean-field model for electrochemical metallization memristors with a nanoporous matrix: application to Ag/anodic alumina/Al](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08724)
*Lucy A. Valdez,Juan D. Ruiz Díaz,Rodolfo H. Romero,Víctor J. Toranzos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出多细丝电化学金属化忆阻器的解析平均场模型，仅用欧姆定律、法拉第定律与基于功率的断裂判据即可闭式给出开关电压、读电流、断裂支路与高压电流，并在Ag/多孔阳极氧化铝/Al器件上验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多细丝电化学金属化忆阻器通常依赖多参数微观模拟或参数难以对应物理量的唯象定律，缺乏兼具解析性与物理可解释性的模型。

Method: 构建受限在纳米多孔基体孔道内Ag细丝群体的解析平均场模型：细丝截面积服从截断正态分布，断裂后留下的间隙服从对数正态分布且中位数随耗散功率增大；间隙闭合使间隙平方分布整体平移。由此得到开关电压、读电流、断裂支路和高压电流的闭式表达式。实验对象为工业级铝制备的Ag/纳米多孔阳极氧化铝(2.4 μm)/Al结构，常温常压测量双极与单极扫描及脉冲响应，间隙弥散度由脉冲后读电流的时间形状确定。

Result: 拟合参数复现了双极响应，包括电压控制型负微分电阻；在不再拟合的条件下预测单极扫描和脉冲响应幅度（呈现状态记忆）。化学沉积大接触的第二样品可由同一模型描述。真空中器件既不开关也无负微分电阻，残余高压电流符合离子跳跃导电。

Conclusion: 该平均场模型以少量具物理意义的参数统一描述多细丝忆阻器的电阻开关行为，具预测能力；真空结果支持吸附水提供对反应，高压残余电流由离子跳跃导电主导。

Abstract: Memristors based on electrochemical metallization with many filaments are usually modeled by microscopic simulations with many parameters or by phenomenological laws whose parameters are hard to relate to physical quantities. We present an analytical mean-field model for a population of Ag filaments confined in the pores of a nanoporous matrix, built on Ohm's law, Faraday's law and a power-based rupture criterion. The filaments have a truncated normal distribution of cross-sections and rupture leaving lognormally distributed gaps whose median grows with the dissipated power. Gap closing shifts the distribution of squared gaps rigidly, and the model therefore admits closed-form expressions for the switching voltage, the read current, the rupture branch and the high-voltage current. We apply it to Ag/nanoporous anodic alumina/Al structures fabricated from industrial-grade aluminum, with a 2.4$\mu$m alumina layer, and measured under ambient conditions. The parameters were fitted to the bipolar sweeps, with the gap dispersion taken from the temporal shape of the read after a pulse. They reproduce the bipolar response, including voltage-controlled negative differential resistance, and predict without refitting the unipolar sweeps and the amplitude of the pulse response, where state memory appears. A second sample, with a larger contact deposited chemically, is described by the same model. In vacuum the devices neither switch nor show negative differential resistance, as expected if adsorbed water supplies the counter reaction; the residual high-voltage current follows ionic hopping conduction.

</details>


### [2] [Growth and fabrication of advanced PbTe-Pb quantum devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08823)
*Zehao Yu,Wenyu Song,Lining Yang,Zeyu Yan,Yuhao Wang,Weizhao Wang,Xinchen He,Ruidong Li,Jiaye Xu,Shuai Yang,Zonglin Li,Shan Zhang,Xiao Feng,Tiantian Wang,Yunyi Zang,Lin Li,Runan Shang,Qi-Kun Xue,Ke He,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套PbTe-Pb马约拉纳器件的新型生长与制备工艺，通过在PbTe纳米线与Pb沉积之间引入Te保护帽层、掩模去除和阴影墙制备等步骤，实现了跨越纳米线—衬底的连续Pb薄膜与高质量原位等效PbTe-Pb界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三端关联实验要求Pb薄膜从PbTe纳米线连续延伸至衬底，但以往PbTe纳米线与衬底掩模在空间上分离，无法满足该要求，限制了器件设计与拓扑量子比特的实现。

Method: 在PbTe生长与Pb沉积之间插入关键工艺：破超高真空前用Te帽层覆盖纳米线顶面以实现非原位加工；随后去除掩模并制备阴影墙；芯片重新装入生长腔后通过热分解去除Te保护层，再沉积Pb薄膜。

Result: 获得了跨越纳米线到衬底的连续Pb薄膜，且尽管经历非原位处理，仍实现了具有"原位等效"质量的洁净PbTe-Pb界面。

Conclusion: 该技术可支撑复杂设计的先进PbTe-Pb量子器件制备，为拓扑量子比特的实现奠定工艺基础。

Abstract: We report recent progress in the growth and fabrication of advanced PbTe-Pb Majorana devices for three-terminal correlation experiments. A critical prerequisite for these devices is a continuous Pb film spanning from the PbTe nanowire to the substrate. In previous studies, PbTe nanowires were spatially separated from the substrate mask, making them unable to fulfill this requirement. To address this issue, we have developed a new growth and fabrication approach. Key fabrication steps are introduced between the PbTe growth and Pb deposition. The top facet of the PbTe nanowire is covered with a Te capping layer before breaking the ultra-high vacuum, enabling ex situ processing for subsequent steps. The mask is then removed, followed by the fabrication of shadow walls. After reloading the chip into the growth chamber, the Te protection layer is removed through thermal decomposition, and the Pb film is subsequently deposited. Notably, a pristine PbTe-Pb interface with an in-situ-equivalent quality can be achieved despite the ex-situ processing. Our technique facilitates the develop of advanced PbTe-Pb quantum devices with complex designs, paving the way toward the realization of topological qubits.

</details>


### [3] [Coulomb screening of the orbital inverse Faraday effect in finite-size diffusive conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09068)
*A.A. Kopasov,A.S. Mel'nikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 从耦合自洽静电场的玻尔兹曼方程出发，建立二维圆盘中逆法拉第效应的动理学理论，数值求解表明库仑屏蔽使二阶直流响应的峰值向高频移动并压低峰值幅度，说明自洽屏蔽对有限尺寸扩散导体的光致直流磁化预测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限尺寸扩散导体中库仑屏蔽对二阶直流响应的影响，澄清非自洽近似描述逆法拉第效应的局限性。

Method: 以玻尔兹曼方程加自洽静电场的动理学理论处理二维圆盘在圆偏振交流电场驱动下的逆法拉第效应；一阶电荷调制与感生电场通过库仑相互作用耦合，自洽一阶解给出二阶直流环流的非线性源；径向问题用角谐波展开与Chebyshev配点法数值求解。

Result: 非自洽近似下，二阶直流响应随频率变化在辐射频率对应的扩散长度与样品半径可比时出现极大值；自洽屏蔽显著修正该行为，使极值移向更高驱动频率并压低峰值幅度，在面积积分环流与感生磁矩上表现一致。

Conclusion: 库仑自洽性对有限尺寸扩散导体中光致直流磁化的定量预测不可或缺，明确了非自洽描述逆法拉第效应的适用边界。

Abstract: We study the effects of Coulomb screening on the second-order dc response in finite-size diffusive conductors. Starting from the Boltzmann equation supplemented by the self-consistent electrostatic field, we develop a kinetic theory of the inverse Faraday effect for a two-dimensional disk driven by a circularly polarized ac electric field. In this formulation, the first-order charge modulation and the induced electric field are coupled through the Coulomb interaction, and the self-consistent first-order solution determines the nonlinear source for the second-order dc circulating current responsible for the induced magnetic moment. The radial problem is solved numerically using an angular-harmonic expansion and the Chebyshev collocation method. It is shown that in the non-self-consistent approximation the frequency dependence of the second-order dc response has a maximum when the diffusion length associated with the radiation frequency becomes comparable to the sample radius. This behavior is found to be strongly modified by self-consistent screening. We demonstrate that Coulomb screening shifts the maximum of the dc response to a higher drive frequency and suppresses the peak amplitude. This behavior is observed consistently for the area-integrated circulating current and the induced magnetic moment. The obtained results clarify the limitations of the non-self-consistent description of the inverse Faraday effect and show that Coulomb self-consistency is crucial for quantitative predictions of optically induced dc magnetization in finite-size diffusive conductors.

</details>


### [4] [Strong phonon-coupled trion emission in Janus homobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09083)
*Tianyishan Sun,Dingkun Bi,Haonan Wang,Hiroto Ogura,Yanlin Gao,Mina Maruyama,Susumu Okada,Shiro Entani,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kazunari Matsuda,Toshiaki Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道S/S堆垛构型的对称双层Janus WSeS中热稳定且增强的三子（trion）发光：向内反平行的内建电场在界面产生强电子限域，主导低温三子发光；与光学声子A_1^2（51 meV）共振的巨大电子-声子耦合强度（3.9）使三子发光在高温下保持热稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janus过渡金属硫族化合物因镜面对称性破缺，为研究自旋-谷-声子相互作用介导的多体效应提供新平台；而强电子-声子耦合要求强有效偶极矩与纳米尺度电荷限域精确匹配，传统半导体体系存在热退相干限制，亟需通过界面结构设计加以突破。

Method: 构建S/S堆垛的对称双层Janus WSeS同质双层结构，利用界面处向内指向的反平行内建电场实现强电子限域；结合变温发光光谱表征三子发射行为，并提取与光学声子A_1^2（51 meV）共振的电子-声子耦合强度。

Result: 低温下界面强电子限域主导三子发光；电子-声子耦合强度高达3.9，与51 meV的A_1^2光学声子能量共振，使三子发射在升温条件下仍保持热稳定与增强。

Conclusion: 通过调控Janus WSeS同质双层的界面架构，可克服传统半导体体系固有的热退相干限制，为室温多体相互作用研究提供新思路。

Abstract: Janus transition metal dichalcogenides with broken mirror symmetry provide a novel platform for exploring many-body effects mediated by spin-valley-phonon interactions. Strong electron-phonon coupling demands a precise match between a strong effective dipole moment and nanometer-scale charge confinement. Here, we report thermally robust and enhanced trion emission in a symmetric bilayer WSeS with an S/S stacking configuration. The inward-pointing antiparallel built-in electric fields induce a strong electron confinement effect at the interface, driving dominant trion emission at cryogenic temperatures. Furthermore, a massive electron-phonon coupling strength (3.9) resonant with the optical phonon A_1^2 energy (51 meV) contributes to the thermal stability of this trion emission at elevated temperatures. By tailoring the interfacial architecture in Janus WSeS homobilayers, this study overcomes the thermal decoherence limits inherent to traditional semiconductor systems, offering novel insights into room-temperature many-body interactions.

</details>


### [5] [Disentangling Spin and Orbital Contributions in the Search for Altermagnetic Terahertz Conversion in RuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09098)
*Jiahao Liu,Yuxiao Mo,Tongyang Guan,Wenhui Fang,Yongwei Cui,Wentao Qin,Zhe Yuan,Lishu Zhang,Zhensheng Tao,Yizheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用太赫兹发射光谱首次在RuO2异质结中实验解耦自旋-电荷与轨道-电荷转换通道，发现Fe主要注入自旋流、Ni与坡莫合金主要注入轨道流，且未观察到逆交错磁自旋劈裂效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO2作为候选交错磁体，其电荷-角动量转换的微观来源尚不明确；近期理论与实验提示轨道输运可能占主导，导致自旋介导与轨道介导转换之间存在根本性歧义。

Method: 采用太赫兹发射光谱，对RuO2异质结中自旋流与轨道流注入进行对照实验，通过不同注入层（Fe、Ni、坡莫合金）区分注入的角动量类型，并分析各向异性响应。

Result: Fe被识别为以自旋流注入为主，Ni与坡莫合金以轨道流注入为主，轨道对超快太赫兹发射有显著贡献；在受控自旋流注入下未发现逆交错磁自旋劈裂效应，观察到的各向异性与受近界面环境强烈调控的各向异性逆自旋霍尔响应一致；轨道流注入下各向异性明显较弱，表明RuO2中轨道-电荷转换基本各向同性。

Conclusion: 轨道输运与界面调控的自旋霍尔转换是RuO2太赫兹发射的关键机制；在将各向异性归因于交错磁性之前，必须解耦自旋、轨道与界面贡献。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 has emerged as a prominent candidate altermagnet, yet the microscopic origin of its reported charge-angular-momentum conversion remains unsettled. Recent theoretical and experimental advances have highlighted a potentially dominant role of orbital transport, raising a fundamental ambiguity between spin- and orbital-mediated conversion in RuO2. Here, we report the first experimental disentanglement of the spin- and orbital-to-charge conversion channels in RuO2 heterostructures using terahertz emission spectroscopy. We identify Fe as a predominantly spin-current injector, whereas Ni and permalloy predominantly inject orbital currents, revealing a substantial orbital contribution to ultrafast terahertz emission. Under controlled spin-current injection, we find no evidence for inverse altermagnetic spin-splitting effect. Instead, the observed anisotropy is consistent with an anisotropic inverse spin Hall response that is strongly conditioned by the near-interface environment. Under orbital-current injection, the anisotropy is markedly weaker, indicating that orbital-to-charge conversion is largely isotropic in RuO2. Our results establish orbital transport and interface-conditioned spin Hall conversion as key to terahertz emission from RuO2, underscoring the need to disentangle spin, orbital, and interfacial contributions before assigning anisotropy to altermagnetism.

</details>


### [6] [Momentum-space engineering in van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09285)
*Xiao Li,Astrid Weston,James G. McHugh,Michael D. Thompson,Matthew Watson,Yunze Gao,Nicholas Clark,Laxman Nagi Reddy,Samuel Magorrian,Wendong Wang,Zhiyuan Wang,Sofia Konyzheva,Francisco Selles,Amy Carl,Katarzyna J. Salamon,Alex Summerfield,Nicholas Hine,Andre K. Geim,Neil R. Wilson,Jonathan R. Prance,Andrey V. Kretinin,Vladimir I. Falko,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出动量空间"Umklapp带工程"新范式：通过倒空间带边匹配在非公度双层中实现共振层间杂化，在扭角石墨烯/MoS2中观测到贝里相位为π的新狄拉克谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯低能谱调控长期依赖实空间超晶格（莫尔微带工程），手段单一，亟需超越莫尔超晶格与弱邻近效应的新调控途径。

Method: 利用两相邻高度非公度二维晶体的布拉格矢量平移使倒空间带边位置重合，触发共振层间杂化并重构能谱；实验上采用与MoS2特定扭角的单层石墨烯，将MoS2 Q谷态经Umklapp过程与石墨烯狄拉克锥在能量-动量上共振杂化，并用朗道能级扇、SdH振荡及位移场依赖测量表征。

Result: 杂化后出现清晰的额外狄拉克型谱特征，表现为特征朗道能级扇与SdH振荡，对应贝里相位π电子；形成自旋-谷锁定的Ising体系，对小扭角涨落稳健，并可由外部位移场进一步调控。

Conclusion: Umklapp带工程为范德华异质结电子结构裁剪提供可行策略，将量子材料设计空间拓展至莫尔超晶格与弱邻近效应之外。

Abstract: Twenty years of graphene research have established Dirac fermions as a versatile platform for exploring quantum topological phenomena. Yet control over graphene's low-energy spectrum mostly follows the route of miniband engineering using real-space superlattices. Here we introduce a complementary approach based on momentum-space 'Umklapp' band engineering. This is realised by matching reciprocal space positions of band edges of two adjacent highly incommensurate 2D crystals to coincide upon a boost from their Bragg vectors, creating resonant interlayer hybridisation that qualitatively reconstructs their spectra. To demonstrate this approach experimentally, we use monolayer graphene on MoS$_2$, twisted such that MoS$_2$ Q-valley states are brought by Umklapp into energy-momentum resonance with graphene's Dirac cones. The ensuing hybridization results in the formation of a clear additional Dirac-like spectral feature, identified by a distinct Landau-level fan and Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations characteristic to Berry phase $\pi$ electrons. These new topological spectral features create a spin-valley locked Ising system which is robust against small twist-angle variations that can be further tuned by an external displacement field. More broadly, Umklapp band engineering offers a practical strategy for tailoring the electronic structure in van der Waals heterostructures, expanding the design space of quantum materials beyond moiré superlattices and weak proximity effects.

</details>


### [7] [Helical magnetism from sliding Luttinger liquids in hole-doped twisted bilayer WTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09462)
*Yung-Yeh Chang,Chen-Hsuan Hsu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究空穴掺杂扭角双层WTe₂中巡游载流子介导的螺旋磁性：以无谷简并的双味滑移Luttinger液体描述载流子，通过通道内螺旋序的反馈机制，通道间自旋极化率中介出RKKY相互作用，稳定跨耦合线的相干螺旋磁织构；自旋波分析给出微波量级的磁振子能量，且螺旋序部分打开载流子能隙并重整化自旋关联与弛豫指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭角双层WTe₂兼具低维性、可调掺杂与局域磁矩，是探索载流子介导磁序的理想平台，但一维/准一维体系中巡游载流子如何通过关联效应稳定长程螺旋磁序仍缺乏清晰的理论图像，磁振子激发与可探测性也不明确。

Method: 采用无谷自由度的双味滑移Luttinger液体描述空穴载流子；利用通道内螺旋序形成的反馈机制计算通道间自旋极化率，由此导出局域磁矩间的RKKY相互作用并分析其对耦合线阵列中相干螺旋磁织构的稳定作用；对有序态进行自旋波分析，估算磁振子能量与螺旋序尺度，并分析螺旋序对载流子谱的能隙化及对自旋关联、弛豫指数的重整化。

Result: 通道间自旋极化率中介的RKKY作用可稳定跨耦合线的相干螺旋磁织构；磁振子能量依赖载流子相互作用，典型值落在微波频段，可由磁共振技术探测；螺旋序部分打开载流子能隙，并重整化自旋关联与弛豫指数。

Conclusion: 扭角双层WTe₂被确认为可实现并探测载流子介导螺旋磁性的可调低维平台，滑移Luttinger液体加反馈机制的理论框架为低维关联体系中磁性、磁振子谱与输运/弛豫性质的关联研究提供了可检验的图像。

Abstract: We investigate carrier-mediated helical magnetism in hole-doped twisted bilayer WTe$_2$ coupled to localized moments. The itinerant carriers are described by a valleyless two-flavor sliding Luttinger liquid. Via the feedback mechanism of the helical order formed within individual channels, we show that the interchannel spin susceptibility mediates a Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida interaction between localized moments that can stabilize a coherent helical magnetic texture across the coupled-wire array. A spin-wave analysis of the ordered state allows us to estimate the magnon energies and helix-ordering scale. The magnon energies depend on the carrier interactions with representative values falling in the microwave regime, suggesting possible detection by magnetic-resonance techniques. The helical order partially gaps the carrier spectrum and renormalizes the spin-correlation and relaxation exponents. These results identify twisted bilayer WTe$_2$ as a tunable low-dimensional platform for realizing and probing carrier-mediated helical magnetism.

</details>


### [8] [Josephson current along the domain walls in Weyl fully compensated ferrimagnet Ti$_2$MnAl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09547)

For the parallel magnetic field, one can see standard Fraunhofer-like $I_c(B)$ suppression pattern. In contrast, $I_c(B)$ is qualitatively different in normal magnetic field, indicating non-trivial distribution of the Josephson current within the junction plane. From this anisotropy, long-range supercurrent and fast aperiodic fluctuations in $I_c(B)$ we conclude that Josephson current is transferred by the chiral surface states, which emerge along the domain walls in topological compensated ferrimagnet Ti$_2$MnAl. Abstract: We propose a protocol for the non-resonant electric control of quantum states in atomic-scale spin systems using Landau-Zener-Stückelberg-Majorana (LZSM) interferometry. We first analyze a Ti dimer in the absence of the Fe atom and show, through a detailed spectroscopic study, that avoided level crossings can be engineered by combining the in-plane component of the external magnetic field, the tip magnetic field, and the applied bias voltage, which allows the system to be tuned electrically through these crossings. Based on this, we develop LZSM protocols for coherent state control in the dimer using non-resonant LZSM-STM. We then consider the full trimer, where the Fe atom generates a local magnetic field at the remote Ti atom, enabling non-local spin control. We show that this remote and non-resonant manipulation reduces unwanted effects associated with tunneling electrons and enables quantum operations on short timescales. Our results show that quantum interference can be used as a resource for electrically driven, non-resonant, and non-local spin control in atomic-scale systems. This approach enables fast quantum operations while mitigating decoherence, offering a promising route toward scalable atom-based qubit architectures.

Abstract: Suspended carbon nanotubes (CNTs) offer promising opportunities for quantum nanomechanics owing to their small effective mass, strong electrostatic tunability, and mechanical nonlinearity. We develop a unified theoretical framework for coherent control and readout of a suspended CNT driven by an atomic force microscope (AFM) tip and capacitively coupled to a Cooper-pair box (CPB). We first derive the linear and nonlinear Duffing response of the CNT and quantify the effects of AFM force gradients, tip-position fluctuations, and voltage noise on mechanical stability, dephasing, and heating. These results establish operating conditions for coherent actuation with limited backaction. We then quantize the Duffing oscillator, derive its Kerr anharmonicity, and identify the requirements for selective control of individual vibrational transitions. Estimates of thermal occupation and active cooling illustrate the constraints for CNT modes spanning frequencies from 54 MHz to 5.37 GHz. Finally, we derive the CNT--CPB interaction and its dispersive limit, showing how phonon number and parity can be mapped onto the CPB through Ramsey interferometry and detected using quantum-capacitance readout. The resulting framework identifies the conditions under which localized AFM actuation, nonlinear quantum dynamics, and superconducting state-resolved detection can operate within a single CNT device.

Abstract: In this work, we try to understand the question of how to address the survival of band topology in the presence of dephasing. We investigate this question in the Kane-Mele $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological insulator. To determine the stability of the topological invariant we construct a single particle Hamiltonian that also provides identical correlation at the single particle level. The phase transition from topological to trivial is determined by the gap closing of this correlation Hamiltonian. This gap closing is related to purity gap. As a diagnostics we calculated the invariant directly and as a complementary diagnostic, coherent two-terminal transport through the auxiliary correlation Hamiltonian exhibits quantized conductance in the nontrivial region. This does not establish physical conductance under dephasing. Our results identify a predictive local criterion for the survival of single-particle topological structure under a specified noise channel.

Abstract: Rydberg excitons in Cu$_2$O provide a solid-state setting for creating giant optical nonlinearities. Analysis reveals an additional enhancement from the resonant second-harmonic cavity giving an overall SHG enhancement factor of $21 \pm 2$ inside the microcavity. These results establish cavity-enhanced SHG spectroscopy as a valuable tool for studying Rydberg excitons in microcavities and provide a pathway towards exploiting optical nonlinearities in Rydberg exciton polariton systems.

Abstract: Excitons carry energy and information through a semiconductor without carrying charge. This makes them attractive for optoelectronics, but also hard to guide. Magnetic skyrmions, on the other hand, are moved by small electrical currents. Here we develop a theory of excitons interacting with a skyrmion in which the electron and the hole are treated as distinct constituents, each with its own spin--orbit coupling. We find that the topology of the skyrmion is transferred to the exciton only when the exchange coupling exceeds the relevant spin--orbit splitting. The exciton then experiences a full flux quantum, with opposite signs in the two valleys. The quantum metric of the spin texture creates a ring-shaped barrier that traps excitons, whereas the topological charge fixes the angular momentum of the trapped states and hence their optical selection rules. Because the trap moves with the skyrmion, excitons can be transported by current pulses, and small skyrmions accommodate a single exciton, which opens the door for potential hybrid applications in future information technology intertwining topological magnetic bits with optical excitations.

Abstract: Polarons are composite quasiparticles formed when charge carriers strongly couple to lattice vibrations, manifesting as carriers dressed by a surrounding cloud of phonons. Although this dressing fundamentally renormalizes the effective mass and mobility of carriers, its impact on electrically neutral exciton polarons in 2D semiconductors has received limited attention. In particular, despite the growing interest in polaronic effects in atomically thin semiconductors, only a few theoretical studies have addressed spectral signatures and renormalized properties of exciton polarons in these materials. Here, we develop a microscopic model to describe exciton polarons in transition metal dichalcogenides. Applying this model to $1s$ acoustic polarons in an MoSe$_2$ monolayer, we reveal a distinct kink in the polaron dispersion, accompanied by a pronounced broadening of its spectral function. These spectral signatures mark a critical transition from a regime of well-defined bound exciton polarons, characterized by a renormalized energy, reduced group velocity, and enhanced effective mass, to a regime dominated by exciton-phonon scattering. Furthermore, by incorporating intervalley exciton-phonon interaction, we demonstrate that the energetic position of momentum-dark excitons strongly modifies the exciton-polaron lifetime. Overall, our work provides a microscopic framework for determining renormalized properties of exciton polarons and establishes experimentally accessible polaron signatures in 2D semiconductors.

Motivation: 大规模半导体量子处理器日益采用基于量子比特shuttling的容错架构，但被shuttling的多自旋逻辑量子比特在时空关联1/f噪声下的退相干与泄漏尚不清楚，亟需理解shuttling轨迹、噪声关联与退相干之间的关系。

Result: 对所有编码普遍地：短距离时并行shuttling抗退相干最优；长程传输时顺序shuttling优于其他协议（确切边界依赖具体器件参数）。该共性可由自旋对的退相干因子解释；给出的显式表达式使实验者仅需测得若干独立的成对退相干因子即可预测多自旋比特性能。

Conclusion: 研究建立了shuttling轨迹、噪声关联与量子比特退相干之间的联系，为可扩展半导体量子处理器中抗噪声shuttling提供指导。

Abstract: Logical qubits encoded in collective states of multiple spins are promising building blocks for large-scale semiconductor quantum processors. As novel fault-tolerant architectures increasingly rely on qubit shuttling, understanding decoherence of the shuttled multi-spin qubits becomes crucial. We analyze fidelity and leakage of the logical qubits dephased by the spatio-temporally correlated $1/f$ noise, considering two-, three-, and four-spin encodings. We study different shuttling protocols and establish that for all encodings generally, parallel shuttling provides the best protection against decoherence at short distances, whereas sequential shuttling outperforms others for long-range transport (although the exact boundaries depend on the specific device parameters). We explain this common behavior in terms of the dephasing factors of pairs of the shuttled spins; we provide explicit expressions for fidelities and leakage, thus enabling experimentalists to predict performance of the multi-spin qubits from only several independently measured pairwise dephasing factors. Our results establish a connection between shuttling trajectories, noise correlations, and qubit decoherence, and provide guidance for achieving noise-resilient shuttling in scalable semiconductor quantum processors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态重杂质在费米海中会引发正交灾变（orthogonality catastrophe），导致量子相干性丧失。本文探究杂质被"规定"匀速运动（无后坐）时，多体粒子-空穴激发如何改变退相干行为，即运动能否作为抑制正交灾变的动力学手段。

Method: 采用精确的泛函行列式（functional-determinant）方法，计算排斥相互作用、无穷重杂质以恒定速度v穿过一维理想费米气体的Ramsey响应与谱函数，完整计入多体粒子-空穴激发；并结合长时间波包分析推导极化子能量与衰减率的解析表达式。

Result: 谱响应对杂质速度呈强非单调依赖：在费米速度附近退相干增强；高速下恢复窄而长寿命的极化子共振。长时间波包分析给出的解析结果在全速度区间定量吻合数值计算：高速时极化子能量趋近平均场值，衰减率随1/v趋于零，谱权重趋近1。

Conclusion: 即使对于完全无后坐的无穷重杂质，快速的规定运动也能提供抑制正交灾变、恢复极化子相干性的动力学途径，表明杂质速度是调控多体退相干的关键参数。

Abstract: We investigate the spectroscopic response of a repulsively interacting, infinitely heavy impurity moving at constant velocity $v$ through a one-dimensional ideal Fermi gas. Using an exact functional-determinant approach, we calculate the Ramsey response and spectral function, fully accounting for many-body particle-hole excitations. We uncover a strongly nonmonotonic dependence on impurity velocity: decoherence is enhanced around the Fermi velocity, whereas sufficiently rapid motion restores a narrow, long-lived polaron resonance. A long-time wave-packet analysis yields analytical expressions for the polaron energy and decay rate over the full velocity range and quantitatively captures the numerical results. At high velocity, the polaron energy approaches the mean-field value, the decay rate vanishes as $1/v$, and the spectral weight approaches unity. Thus, rapid prescribed motion provides a dynamical route to suppressing the orthogonality catastrophe and restoring polaron coherence even for an infinitely heavy impurity, where recoil is entirely absent.

</details>

Abstract: We demonstrate sequential single-atom-resolved imaging of the same ultracold atomic sample and apply this technique to quantum-walk measurements in an optical lattice. In quantum gas microscope experiments, fluorescence imaging provides microscopic information on atomic distributions but induces motional heating through photon scattering, making it difficult to reuse the same sample for subsequent quantum dynamics. To overcome this limitation, we apply Raman sideband cooling with parameters optimized for post-imaging cooling, enabling sequential single-atom-resolved measurements of the same sample. By optimizing the cooling parameters with machine learning, we achieve high fidelity over multiple imaging cycles. To demonstrate the capability of this technique, we measure one-dimensional quantum walks of single atoms in an optical lattice. By determining the initial atomic positions from fluorescence images, we extract quantum-walk transition probabilities without requiring deterministic preparation of the initial atomic positions. The observed dynamics agree with numerical simulations of quantum walks of single atoms. Our method provides a route toward reducing experimental overhead in quantum gas microscope studies requiring repeated acquisition of spatially resolved correlation data.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 加载于1D光晶格的Raman耦合玻色气体可实现"Raman任意子-Hubbard模型"，在小统计相位下退化为任意子-Hubbard模型，超越该极限则定义一类具有无界统计相互作用的格点任意子模型；该气体展现密度依赖的动量分布不对称、相互作用诱导的非对称输运与手性束缚态等1D任意子特征，并可用⁃³⁹K原子实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子-Hubbard模型是1D任意子的范本格点模型，其连续极限（手性BF理论）已被证明是Raman耦合、相互作用失衡的玻色气体的低能描述；作者希望把这一连续理论"格点化"，从而在实验可及的冷原子体系中研究超越少粒子区域的1D任意子物理。

Result: 数值结果显示该缀饰气体具备1D任意子的三大判据性特征：(i) 基态动量分布的密度依赖不对称性；(ii) 相互作用诱导的非对称输运；(iii) 手性束缚态。这些特征在Raman耦合⁃³⁹K原子体系中可直接观测。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Raman任意子-Hubbard模型作为手性BF理论的格点对应，并揭示小相位极限之外的一类新格点任意子模型（无界统计相互作用）；其所预言效应可在现有⁃³⁹K冷原子平台实现，为在多粒子区域实验探索1D任意子开辟途径。

Abstract: The anyon-Hubbard model is the paradigmatic lattice model of one-dimensional (1D) anyons, in which chiral interactions make bosons equivalent to particles with fractional statistics. Its continuum limit, the chiral BF theory, emerges as the low-energy description of a Raman-coupled Bose gas with imbalanced interactions. Here, we show that loading such a gas into a 1D optical lattice realizes the lattice counterpart of this theory, which we name the Raman anyon-Hubbard model. The Raman-dressed bosons acquire density-dependent Peierls phases when tunneling through occupied sites. For a small statistical phase, the model reduces to the anyon-Hubbard model, while beyond this limit it defines a distinct lattice anyon model with unbounded rather than periodic statistical interactions. Numerically, we show that the dressed gas displays the defining properties of 1D anyons, namely a density-dependent asymmetry of the ground-state momentum distribution, interaction-induced asymmetric transport and chiral bound states. Remarkably, these features are readily accessible with Raman-coupled $^{39}$K atoms, opening up the experimental exploration of 1D anyons beyond the few-particle regime.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对 (1+1) 维和 (2+1) 维键 DP 构型训练监督式 Transformer 编码器；用分类概率估计临界点与临界指数；采用 attention rollout 分析内部注意力，并定义注意力中心（累积注意力分布的时间质心）；对比完全占据初态与单种子初态两类数据间的迁移，并比较微调与从头训练的精度。

Result: Transformer 能以高准确率区分活跃相与吸收相；由分类概率估计的临界点与临界指数与文献参考值吻合良好；注意力模式与注意力中心在临界区附近系统性重组，并呈明显的有限尺寸依赖；两类初态数据间存在可迁移信息，但迁移性能具有方向不对称性；在同等目标域数据量下，微调相对从头训练仅有边际精度提升。

Conclusion: 监督式 Transformer 可作为从时空构型中提取 DP 临界标度信息的有效工具，其输出概率与内部注意力表征均对临界行为敏感；同时，跨初态迁移虽存在但方向不对称，微调收益有限，提示表征的共享性与任务/数据分布差异需进一步厘清。

Abstract: Directed percolation (DP) provides a canonical framework for studying nonequilibrium phase transitions into absorbing states, yet extracting critical behavior directly from high-dimensional spatiotemporal configurations remains challenging. Here, we investigate (1 + 1)- and (2 + 1)-dimensional bond DP using a supervised Transformer encoder. Supervised learning results demonstrate that the Transformer can distinguish between the active and absorbing phases of directed percolation with high accuracy. The critical point and critical exponents estimated from the model's classification probabilities align closely with reference values from existing literature, indicating that the model not only achieves phase classification but also yields outputs containing information related to critical scaling behavior. To probe the model's internal representation, we employ attention rollout and define an attention center that quantifies the temporal centroid of the cumulative attention distribution. The attention patterns and the attention center reorganize systematically near the critical region and exhibit pronounced finite-size dependence. Finally, we examined the model's transferability between configurations generated from fully occupied initial states and those from single-seed initial states. The results indicate the presence of transferable information between the two types of data, though the transfer performance exhibits directional asymmetry. Furthermore, when using an equal amount of target-domain data, fine-tuning yielded only a marginal improvement in accuracy compared to training from scratch.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作只给出平均效率，缺乏对效率围绕均值涨落的统计刻画。作者希望建立效率涨落与熵产生之间的微观联系，并用非平衡涨落理论定量说明纳米结构太阳能电池效率为何在统计上稳定。

Method: 一是从描述吸收、辐射复合与提取的两态主方程出发，经局域细致平衡引入热力学亲和势，推导效率的微观随机模型；二是引用近期文献中的普适涨落定律（总熵产生为下鞅、其负指数为鞅、以及有偏离散随机游走的精确下确界定律），首次代入纳米结构太阳能电池的真实参数进行数值验证与模拟。

Result: 总熵产生表现为下鞅，其负指数为鞅；下确界定律在近平衡区数值验证成立，并应用于真实电池构型。模拟显示器件处于强非平衡区，负熵涨落（相应效率涨落）指数稀有，多数构型在所有模拟轨迹中检测不到可观测的负涨落，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该框架在概念与定量层面解释了纳米结构太阳能电池效率涨落，说明其平均效率的统计稳健性源于强非平衡下的涨落律；但并未改变此前得到的平均效率数值。

Abstract: We study nonequilibrium fluctuations of efficiency in nanostructured solar cells. Our contribution is threefold. First, we derive a microscopic stochastic model of efficiency from a two-state master equation describing absorption, radiative recombination, and extraction. This derivation establishes an explicit link between efficiency fluctuations and entropy production, and justifies the thermodynamic affinity through local detailed balance. Second, we review universal fluctuation laws established in the recent literature and apply them for the first time to the real parameters of nanostructured solar cells. We show that the total entropy production behaves as a submartingale, and that its negative exponential is a martingale. For a biased discrete random walk, the exact infimum law is verified numerically in the near-equilibrium regime, then applied to realistic solar cell configurations. Third, our simulations show that nanostructured solar cells operate in a strongly nonequilibrium regime, where negative entropy fluctuations, and hence efficiency fluctuations, are exponentially rare. Over all simulated trajectories, most configurations show no detectable negative fluctuation, consistent with the theoretical prediction. These results explain the intrinsic statistical stability of the devices and provide a conceptual and quantitative framework for understanding efficiency fluctuations around the mean values. They do not modify the mean efficiencies obtained previously, but clarify their statistical robustness in the strongly nonequilibrium regime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明反馈环的置换结构在普适误差内决定盆地熵；尽管环独立振荡，相位偏移导致对可加性的偏离可随 m 线性增长；然而在系综层面，既有的加性预测可保持至第一个次领头项，偏差仅出现在 ln m·ln ln m 阶。

Conclusion: 盆地熵由反馈环置换结构决定（模普适误差），单个网络层面加性可被线性破坏，但系综平均下加性预测在次领头精度上依然成立，偏差层级为 ln m·ln ln m。

Abstract: Basin entropy measures uncertainty about a network's long-term behavior. For every critical one-input synchronous Boolean network with $m$ relevant nodes, we show that the permutation structure induced by its feedback loops determines basin entropy, up to a universal error. Although the loops oscillate independently, their relative phases can be offset, producing a deviation from additivity that can grow linearly in $m$. Yet at the ensemble level, a previously derived additive prediction is preserved through the first subleading term, with the discrepancy appearing at order $\ln m\ln\ln m$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 粒子间相互作用是调控多粒子稀有事件动力学与首达统计的关键因素，可通过调节相互作用势的性质与强度来加速或延缓系统的全局能量极小化过程。

Abstract: We consider the statistics of pairs of particles moving in a potential energy landscape exhibiting a choice of different pathways from a local minimum start point to the global energy minimum destination. We examine the impact of both attractive and repulsive interactions between the particles, treating these via either a soft Gaussian or a Lennard-Jones potential. We investigate the statistics of which particle arrives at the destination first, arrival-time distributions and where in the system the second particle is located when the first arrives. We find that the typical arrival time of the first particle can be significantly different to that of a single particle, sometimes being much longer and in other cases being shorter, depending on the nature of the particle interactions. In the case of soft Gaussian attractive interactions, which effectively bind the pair of particles together to form a single larger particle, the arrival time can be much longer, since bigger particles cross barriers more slowly. In contrast, the arrival time is shorter when there is a repulsive core interaction between the particles, due to excluded-area and particle-ordering effects. We investigate the probability distribution of particles' positions through the Jensen--Shannon divergence which unveils the dependence of the spatial distribution of the particles within the confining potential on the parameters of the particle interaction potentials.

Motivation: 随机符号的交换积分(±J无序)通常使自旋玻璃模型难以严格求解。作者希望借助Majorana表示揭示该无序是否可被规范自由度吸收，从而获得可解析处理的二次型理论，并确定自旋玻璃基态的性质。

Conclusion: ±J Edwards–Anderson模型在横场中存在从顺磁相到自旋玻璃基态的二阶相变，临界横场为Δ=J√Z；自旋玻璃基态在自旋构型上简并，并在T=0时满足replica对称性。

We show that, in this representation, the quantity describing the coupling between neighboring spins commutes with the Hamiltonian and, unlike in the Kitaev model, is not subject to any Gauss law. The resulting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge freedom allows the disorder in the signs of the exchange integrals to be eliminated by an exact gauge transformation, so that the spectrum of the system coincides with that of the model with uniform couplings. The resulting theory is quadratic in the Majorana fields. Computing the local Green's functions, we find that the Edwards--Anderson order parameter vanishes for $\Delta>J\sqrt{Z}$ and equals $1/4$ for $\Delta<J\sqrt{Z}$, which signals a second-order transition from a paramagnetic phase to a spin-glass ground state that is degenerate over spin configurations and replica symmetric at $T=0$.

Abstract: We present here a numerical study of the Toda lattice Hamiltonian dynamics showing how, despite model integrability, it is possible to find many macroscopic observables which display standard equilibrium behavior. In particular, we study numerically the energy time correlation of certain coarse-grained variables, showing that it decays with either exponential or stretched exponential behaviour, thus indicating good thermalization properties. The spirit of our observation is complementary to that of standard Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou-like numerical experiments, in which the breaking of integrability is regarded as the key ingredient for relaxation to thermal equilibrium. The present communication corroborates, on the contrary, the view that integrability is not an obstacle to thermalization, provided that a sufficiently weak definition of the latter is accepted. In the Toda lattice we have introduced several kinds of coarse-grained variables, constructed with the purpose to maintain a thermodynamic relevance also for large system sizes, which all show good thermalization properties.

Abstract: The Mpemba effect is a counterintuitive phenomenon in which two equilibrium systems at different temperatures, say hot and warm systems, when quenched at the same bath temperature, for some pair of hot and warm temperatures, the hot system comes to bath temperature faster than the warm system. In recent years, studies on the Mpemba effect for Markovian systems under external potential, which are continuous-time, continuous-state-space Markov processes, have been carried out theoretically and experimentally by resorting to pseudo-distance measures which are not true distance metrics. Moreover, maximum crossover time is given for the Mpemba effect for all monotone distance measures or functions (which include true distance metrics and pseudo-distance measures). However, no mathematical analysis is carried out for the mere possibility of the presence of the Mpemba effect for Markovian systems under an external potential with a true distance metric. In this paper, by using a true distance metric which arises naturally from the weighted Hilbert space in which the adjoint of the generator of the Markov process is a Hermitian operator, we have shown the mathematical analysis of the possibility of the presence of the Mpemba effect by using Poincaré inequality and the geometry of Hilbert space.

</details>

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

Result: 方法在 SU(2)_1 WZW 临界自旋链上得到验证：不同对称性扇区的协方差本征值服从预期的幂律衰减，拟合得到的标度维数与场论预言一致，能够同时分辨出相关的主算子、边缘主算子以及若干后代（descendant）算子，说明协方差谱确实携带了多算子的标度信息。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵谱是一种普适且实用的标度维数探针：理论上由一般性证明支撑，实践上易于嵌入现有 QMC 等数值方案，可一次性提取多个（含相关、边缘及后代）算子的标度维数，有望显著增强量子临界性的数值研究，特别是对近简并算子谱的辨别。

Using quantum Monte Carlo simulations to compute covariance matrices in different symmetry sectors, we resolve the expected relevant and marginal primary operators as well as some descendants. The covariance method can be integrated into many existing numerical schemes and promises to significantly enhance computational studies of quantum criticality.

Abstract: The zero-magnetic-field metal-insulator transition in strongly correlated two-dimensional electron systems was experimentally discovered three decades ago, thereby triggering extensive experimental and theoretical investigations. In this work, we provide a comprehensive review of the transport and thermodynamic properties of two-dimensional electron and hole systems in which carrier-carrier interactions constitute the dominant energy scale. We present and critically analyze both experimental observations and theoretical developments accumulated over the past thirty years.

Abstract: The sign problem in path integrals severely limits the application of quantum Monte Carlo methods to strongly correlated systems, whereas the Lefschetz-thimble method offers a rigorous framework to mitigate this issue. In this paper, we establish a numerical framework based on the Lefschetz thimbles for the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model. By introducing continuous auxiliary fields via the Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation, the partition function is formulated as a high-dimensional complex integral. We employ the complex Langevin equation to explore the saddle-point distribution of the effective action in the complex plane. Monte Carlo integration is then performed to compute the partition function and Green functions. Our numerical results exhibit excellent agreement with high-temperature expansions, infrared-limit solutions, and determinantal quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) simulations, confirming the validity of our framework. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our complex Langevin search successfully captures the relevant dominant saddle points governing the SYK model in the large-N limit. Our work deepens the understanding of the sign problem in the SYK model and provides a viable non-perturbative avenue for addressing sign problems in more complex strongly correlated systems.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between antiferromagnetism and topological superconductivity in twisted cuprate bilayers. With no obstruction beyond this limit, our method obtains direct estimates of order parameters. For the model near $J_2/J_1=0.5$, and the kagome lattice Heisenberg model, our data indicate linear-dispersion gapless states. To probe the candidate spin-liquid nature of the highly frustrated cases, we go beyond inferring the absence of symmetry-breaking orders and analyze susceptibilities that characterize gapless spin liquids. By estimating scaling dimensions, our data indicate a stable gapless spin liquid on the kagome lattice, and competing scenarios for the intermediate regime of the square-lattice model. Our developments include parallelization and randomized low-rank methods and largely expand the frontier of high-precision variational numerics for quantum phases of matter.

Abstract: In Heo and Kiem (2019), the authors raised a problem relating integral zeros of binary Krawtchouk polynomials, parametrized by quadruples $(m,n,k,\ell)$ with $m\le n$ and $k\le \ell$, to the existence of pairs of subspaces satisfying the range criterion in quantum information theory, and settled the symmetric case $k=\ell$. In this paper, we solve the problem for the following three asymmetric families:
 $(m,n,k,\ell)=(3,t^2-1,t(t-1)/2,t(t+1)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
 $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3t^2+2t-1,(3t^2-t)/2,(3t^2+5t+2)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
 $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3s^2+4s,(3s^2+s)/2,(3s^2+7s+4)/2)$, $s\ge1$.
 The remaining cases are sporadic zeros, with only finitely many for each $m\ge5$.

</details>

Abstract: Rapid thermodynamic operations generally entail irreversible dissipation, giving rise to trade-offs between operating speed and thermodynamic cost. Yet, how quantum feedback fundamentally reshapes these trade-offs remains elusive. Here, we derive a finite-time current--dissipation relation for continuously monitored open quantum dynamics with jump feedback. From this fundamental relation, we establish a quantum speed limit and a power-efficiency trade-off for stationary information engines, identifying how feedback affects state transformations and the performance of engines through both informational entropy and direct dynamical contributions. Our results provide a quantitative foundation for fast, low-dissipation quantum thermodynamics in the presence of feedback control, exemplified by attaining the generalized Landauer bound in finite-time information erasure and approaching unit information efficiency at finite power in the large-system limit of a collective engine.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 由极值化magic得到的物理结论可能依赖度量选择；在高能/核物理中仅以SRE为依据外推结论需谨慎，应明确度量并考察其解析性质。

Abstract: Magic, or nonstabilizerness, is the quantum resource behind universal quantum computation. Its applications in high-energy and nuclear physics rely almost exclusively on the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), although magic has no unique measure. For two qubits, we compare the SRE with three measures defined through optimizations over stabilizer states: the min- and max-relative entropies of magic and the logarithmic free robustness. We determine the maximal-magic states of all four measures, with exact proofs, and find that they depend on the measure. In the determination of the weak mixing angle from minimal magic in Møller scattering, the min-relative entropy gives an energy-dependent value that differs from that of the SRE, whereas the max-relative entropy and the logarithmic free robustness give $\sin^2\theta_W=1/4$ at all energies. We trace this difference to the non-analytic structure of the optimization-based measures. Physical conclusions drawn from extremizing magic can thus depend on the measure.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider a class of quantum circuits, known as quantum many-body spin ratchets, which act on alternating spin chains with two distinct spin types on even and odd sites. This setup breaks parity symmetry and exhibits chiral transport. It has been shown that the drift velocity in the case of integrable, SU(2)-symmetric quantum spin ratchets is universal, and conjectured that the same holds in the non-integrable case. Here, we prove that this universal drift is exact at all times, requiring only translation invariance and U(1) symmetry. Furthermore, we prove that it is sufficient for the circuit to be translation-invariant on average by deriving the same result in the random case. Finally, we demonstrate that breaking translation invariance immediately results in deviations from this universal prediction.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文数值研究图态量子费舍尔信息(QFI)是否超过标准量子极限(SQL)与图团复形Betti数之间的关系，发现拓扑优化可导向超越SQL的图态，但Betti数本身并不决定QFI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图态的QFI有闭式表达（计数具有相同邻域的顶点对），但尚不清楚哪些全局结构性质决定QFI能否超越SQL；作者考察团复形Betti数这类拓扑不变量是否能刻画该行为。

Result: 增边最终同时破坏计量优势与非平凡拓扑；删边时高阶Betti数在QFI已降至SQL后仍长期存在。蒙特卡洛搜索将随机图推向近极端多部结构，其QFI显著超越SQL。

Conclusion: 拓扑优化是获得计量有用图态的一条途径，但Betti数单独不能决定QFI，QFI超越SQL与否不能仅由拓扑不变量判定。

Abstract: Graph states are a versatile resource for quantum metrology, and their quantum Fisher information (QFI) has a closed-form expression in terms of the underlying graph: it counts pairs of vertices with identical neighborhoods. It remains unclear, however, which global structural properties of a graph determine whether its QFI exceeds the standard quantum limit (SQL). Here we ask whether topological invariants, namely the Betti numbers of the graph's clique complex, capture this behavior, and we address the question numerically with two complementary approaches. First, we randomly add or delete edges in balanced complete multipartite graphs, which combine a QFI beyond the SQL with a single nonvanishing higher Betti number. Edge addition eventually destroys both the metrological advantage and the nontrivial topology. Under edge deletion, by contrast, higher Betti numbers persist well after the QFI has dropped to the SQL. Second, we start from Erdős--Rényi random graphs, whose QFI does not typically exceed the SQL, and maximize a chosen Betti number with a Monte Carlo search. The search drives the graphs toward near-extremal multipartite structures whose QFI lies well beyond the SQL. Our results show that topological optimization offers a route to metrologically useful graph states, but also that Betti numbers alone do not determine the QFI.

</details>

Abstract: Semi-device-independent quantum protocols often rely on strict Hilbert-space dimension assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in realistic implementations, particularly with infinite-dimensional optical fields. The almost-qudit framework addresses this issue by quantifying the leakage of quantum states beyond a target subspace. Although such leakage can severely compromise randomness certification for fixed preparation and measurement inputs, we show that averaging over the full input ensemble can mitigate this vulnerability and extend the range of tolerable leakage. This enhanced robustness enables nonzero randomness certification using low-squeezing single-mode Gaussian states and homodyne detection, thus offering an experimentally accessible continuous-variable implementation.

</details>

Abstract: Gravity is by far the weakest among all known interactions. While it dominates the large-scale structure of the universe, gravitational interactions between small masses in the microscopic range remain underexplored because they are too weak to be experimentally accessible. The interface between gravity, described by general relativity, and quantum mechanics, which describes the microscopic world, is of particular interest. From a theory standpoint, these two major descriptions of nature have not been combined in a satisfactory manner. Technology is gradually becoming mature enough such that probing gravity at the microscopic scale, where quantum mechanics and gravity potentially meet, is becoming accessible. Explorations towards genuine quantum gravity have to be carried out under conditions where the gravitating masses possess non-classical properties, which necessitates ultimate protection against disturbances, such as ultralow temperatures and a small range of motion. Here, we present an experiment at millikelvin temperatures, where the gravity due to a micron-range coherent displacement of an 87 mg source mass imprints on the motion of an oscillating membrane loaded with a 1.3 mg test mass. Vibrations of the membrane are measured in the microwave optomechanical scheme, which is a key setup for investigating non-classical motional states of mechanical oscillators. When considering the combination of total source and test masses, our setup presents two orders of magnitude progress in sensing gravity at the small scale, as well as a 35 dB improvement of gravitational sensitivity at kHz frequencies or above. Our setup offers a plausible approach for studying gravitational interactions between masses prepared in non-classical motional states.

</details>

*Kenneth Goodenough*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部补运算表面上是一种奇特的图操作，却因描述图态的局部Clifford等价而成为图态研究的基本概念；然而纯图论描述难以揭示稳定子态的结构性质，作者希望找到更本质的刻画工具。

Result: 基与切换的视角揭示了仅靠图论描述难以获得的稳定子态结构性质，如vertex-minor及局部Pauli测量统计；同时简化了若干已有证明，并提供多个示例。

Conclusion: 基是多拟阵研究的基础概念，本文亦作为将多拟阵应用于量子信息理论的入门性介绍。

Abstract: A local complementation at a vertex $v$ of a graph $G$ replaces the induced neighborhood of $v$ with its complement. Local complementations are, at face value, peculiar operations. Yet they famously show up when describing the local Clifford equivalence among graph states, and, because of this, have become a fundamental notion in the study of graph states. Following Bouchet, we show here how local complementations on graphs are more naturally thought of in terms of switchings of (matroid) bases of stabilizer states. Bases and switchings reveal structural properties of stabilizer states that are harder to glean from the graph-theoretic description alone, such as vertex-minors and statistics of local Pauli measurements. Bases also simplify prior proofs about graphs up to local complementations, of which we give several examples. Bases are foundational in the study of (multi)matroids; as such this paper serves as a gentle introduction to the applications of multimatroids to quantum information theory.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRC只训练线性读出，其状态不可直接观测，特征均为有限次测量估计的期望值，故测量次数（shot预算）成为无经典对应的一级超参数。已有研究开始考察其影响，但"所需预算"如何定义尚不明确，且不同定义是否一致未被澄清。

Conclusion: 应以固定测量预算下的性能作为配置比较准则，而非各配置达到自身收敛上限所需预算；测量预算应被显式视为核心超参数。

Its effect on QRC has recently begun to be studied; what remains unsettled is how a required budget should be defined, and whether competing definitions agree. This paper argues that they do not, and that the disagreement is systematic rather than incidental.

Proposition~\ref{prop:confound} shows that $N_\star$ depends on the noise-free error floor by construction, so that a configuration with a worse floor appears cheaper, and that configurations should therefore be compared at a fixed measurement budget rather than by the budget each needs to reach its own ceiling.

</details>

Recursive QAOA (RQAOA) scales further by using the resulting correlations, whether variables tend to agree or disagree, to eliminate variables one at a time. But each inferred sign is accepted irrevocably, so a single early mistake propagates through every later reduction and can discard the global optimum. As elimination proceeds, residuals shrink while effective edge density typically rises, so later decisions face denser Ising problems, a regime that fully connected Sherrington Kirkpatrick (SK) instances stress test directly. This paper proposes sign-hedged beam RQAOA, which retains the variable or pair selected by standard RQAOA, explores both possible sign assignments, keeps the best B residual problems according to a classical score, and completes each survivor with ordinary RQAOA. On frustrated SK instances, where standard recursion collapses to AR 0.474, beam recovers near-optimal AR 0.998 at the same p. We evaluate the workflow on 50+ instances from HamLib and QOBLIB, spanning 4 and 5 regular and Erdos Renyi MaxCut graphs, Sherrington Kirkpatrick (SK) spin glasses, and cardinality-constrained portfolio optimization, with up to 60 qubits and hardware runs on ibm kingston and ibm marrakesh. Matched simulator and device cells show consistent beam rankings: simulation and hardware agree that beam recovers near-optimal approximation ratio (AR) where standard recursion fails. These gains come from additional residual-circuit evaluations, classical pruning, and exact solution of a small core, rather than from increasing p, beam is a decision-side optimization atop fixed-p QAOA/RQAOA, not a claim that quantum circuits alone scale with qubit count.

Conclusion: 随机编码加逐出是一种高效、可验证的构造范式，能系统性超越现有码表在"最佳已知码不强于随机码"区域的记录，并获得精确距离认证；一阶矩分析提供了可预测的增益来源与后续搜索方向。

Abstract: I report 192 explicit binary stabilizer codes $[[n,k,d]]$ with $53\le n\le 254$ and $5\le d\le 13$ whose minimum distance exceeds the lower bound listed in Grassl's code tables at the same $(n,k)$: 161 codes by one and 31 by two. Through the standard propagation rules they raise 1,873 entries of the table. For 190 of the codes, a random Clifford encoder is followed by expurgation: every logical operator of weight below the target distance is enumerated, and a few logical operators are promoted to stabilizers so that each low-weight one anticommutes with at least one of them. The other two are obtained by puncturing a code from the table and repairing it in the same way. All 192 distances are exact: the lower bound is certified by exhaustive meet-in-the-middle enumeration of up to $4\times10^{13}$ Pauli operators, and the upper bound by an explicit logical operator of weight $d$. A first-moment count explains the results. Expurgated random codes have about eight more logical qubits than a typical random code of the same length and distance, so they beat the table wherever its best known codes are no better than random. The largest gains, up to nine logical qubits, are at $d\ge9$ and $n\gtrsim170$, where the table often gives a lower bound without an explicit code. The same count predicts improvements at 619 further pairs $(n,d)$. The search was run by AI agents. The codes, their certificates and an independent checker are public.

Abstract: Structural descriptors provide graph neural networks (GNNs) with positional and topological inductive biases. However, widely used classical encodings based on random walks or Laplacian spectra emphasize specific structural statistics of a graph, leaving room for alternative descriptors. In this work, we use these measurements as quantum graph features (QGFs), providing a complementary, hardware-derived family of structural encoding that is not accurately described by classical features derived from Laplacian and adjacency matrices, even when supplemented with an extended set of random-walk, geometric descriptors and classical physical models. On an intrinsic information benchmark of 15 topological and spectral probing tasks, quantum and hybrid quantum-classical features match or surpass classical descriptors on local topological properties. When supplied to message-passing, attention, and graph-transformer backbones, hybrid quantum-classical features yield improvements that are within cross-fold noise on most cells but sign-consistent across architectures. When comparing noiseless, shot-sampled emulation-derived quantum features with a neutral-atom QPU implementation, the downstream hybrid results with compressed hardware features match their emulated counterparts within $0.01$ F$_1$ on all backbones. On a synthetic family of ladder graphs, QGFs distinguish two specific classes, outperforming the strongest classical structural descriptors by a margin exceeding cross-fold variability.

Abstract: When deriving the expression for the Noether's current one obtains a term that is an integral over the Euler-Lagrange equation. This term vanishes along the classical path and so does not contribute to the current. However, quantum particles do not necessarily follow the classical path; this concept is the basis of Feynman's path integral quantization formalism. Here, we compute this quantum fluctuation from Noether's theorem and the path integral formalism. We find that, for a time invariant system, the expression for the quantum fluctuations is give by minus the functional expression for the Hamiltonian, $\Delta E = -H(y)$, evaluated at the distance from the classical path, $y$. As an example we evaluate quantum energy fluctuations of a single Harmonic oscillator. In the short time limit we recover the energy-time uncertainty relation and in the long time limit we recover the zero point energy of the quantum harmonic oscillator.

Abstract: Perfect wave transfer (PWT) reproduces spatial reflection at a common finite time under a fixed Hamiltonian. At low temperature, an expansion in the detuning response gives a leading estimate of the noise amplitude at which this deficit first appears. Abstract: Recent experimental demonstrations of Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) have attracted significant attention for their ability to establish information-theoretically secure encryption keys for next-generation communication systems. As practical deployment accelerates, the realization of compact, scalable, and high-throughput discrete-variable (DV) QKD systems has become increasingly important. While advances in photonic integration are enabling low-size, low-weight, and low-power QKD hardware, the security and performance of these systems rely heavily on efficient post-processing techniques to generate identical, secure keys. This tutorial presents a comprehensive algorithm-level review of practical post-processing techniques for DV-QKD, with particular emphasis on information reconciliation methods based on Cascade, Winnow, low-density parity-check (LDPC), and polar codes. We also analyze the theoretical asymptotic and finite-size secret key rates (SKRs) of DV-QKD and benchmark these bounds against the practical SKRs achieved using actual post-processing algorithms. Furthermore, we present a server-client model to implement the entire QKD post-processing pipeline. The performance is evaluated in terms of QBER and SKR across various system parameters, considering the complete post-processing pipeline, including sifting, information reconciliation, privacy amplification, and authentication. This tutorial also presents a graphical user interface (GUI) for the QKD post-processing methods, enabling researchers, industry practitioners, and academic users to visualize and analyze the impact of different post-processing techniques and system parameters, such as QBER, on the SKR performance of DV-QKD systems.

Abstract: Krylov subspace methods efficiently approximate extremal eigenstates and eigenvalues of many-body Hamiltonians, but non-orthogonal bases can suffer from ill-conditioning. We investigate a variational Krylov-like framework that iteratively optimizes a parametrized sequence of states to mimic Lanczos-like couplings while penalizing linear dependencies. Analyzed against exact Krylov and Lanczos on a finite Anderson impurity model, ansatz expressivity strongly influences the performance: Hamiltonian-inspired ansätze preserve Krylov-like convergence while improving the conditioning, whereas shallow ansätze remain stable but miss low-energy directions.

Abstract: How a quantum resource can be quantified depends on which operations are free. We show reachability from every state orthogonal to the textureless state, whether pure or mixed: each can be freely transformed into any other state. Therefore, every texture monotone must assign the same maximal value to all such states, a requirement that neither candidate satisfies. We then construct valid texture monotones by adding the population outside the textureless state to the skew information and by maximizing the squared Hilbert-Schmidt distance over free operations. </details>

Abstract: Quantum reservoir processing (QRP) has been applied to entanglement quantification, yet its potential for generalizable steering detection and the discovery of high-dimensional quantum correlations remains largely unexplored. Some CEQRP-based classifiers substantially improve cross-family generalization, with logistic regression trained on full-information CEQRP features achieving approximately $96\%$ accuracy on unseen Werner states and over $97\%$ accuracy on unseen isotropic states at both \(m=4\) and \(m=5\). Independently, we establish two interconnected steering criteria for mixed two-qutrit states: a necessary-and-sufficient all-versus-nothing criterion for states admitting three linearly independent pure conditional states, and an asymptotic sufficient criterion, with the two criteria linked through invertible local filtering. These criteria provide rigorously certified steerable samples for training-set augmentation. Combining CEQRP-based classification with these theory-certified samples enables the discovery of new Alice-to-Bob steerable partially entangled states, including states at \(p=0.45\) whose steerability is independently confirmed by semidefinite programming (SDP) using seven projective measurement settings. The trained classifiers further guide finite-setting searches for steering boundaries. Our results establish a pathway from generalizable classification to the discovery of high-dimensional quantum steering.

</details>


### [62] [A Geometric Journey Through the Aharonov--Bohm Effect: Tonomura's Toroid, Pseudoscalar Phase, and Quantum Holonomy in Clifford Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09311)
*Andrew Powell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用实欧氏Clifford代数Cl(3,0)重新表述Tonomura型超导屏蔽环形磁体中的磁Aharonov–Bohm效应：将电磁U(1)相位表示为伪标量I生成的中心单位多重矢量e^{Iα}（而非空间转子），内蕴地给出通量、Stokes定理、完整回路holonomy U_Γ=exp(IqΦ_B/ħ)及符号，并与超导fluxoid量子化衔接得到理想极限下的二值电子holonomy U_Γ=(-1)^N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁AB效应多以理想无限长螺线管和复标量相位引入，几何上不够贴近真实实验装置（Tonomura电子全息实验中超导屏蔽的紧凑环形磁体），且相位、通量与符号约定常被含糊处理。作者希望在一个统一、内蕴的几何代数框架中，明确区分不同阶（grade）的几何对象，并以规范的U(1)协变方式严格固定所有符号。

Method: 在Cl(3,0)中区分单位体积伪标量I(3阶)、坐标角(0阶)、切矢量(1阶)与有向面双矢量(2阶)；磁场用对偶双矢量ℬ=I B表示，电磁U(1)相位用中心单位多重矢量e^{Iα}表示（其生成元为伪标量I，而非空间转子的双矢量生成元）。建立联动环面几何（双环链接），在几何代数中内蕴表述磁通量与Stokes定理，并依据U(1)规范协变性导出holonomy元U_Γ=exp(IqΦ_B/ħ)，逐一显式确定所有符号；随后以同一记号表达超导fluxoid量子化。

Result: 得到与实验几何相符的内蕴通量与Stokes表述，及显式符号固定的holonomy元U_Γ=exp(IqΦ_B/ħ)；在理想极限（链接磁通Φ_B=N h/(2e)）下导出二值电子holonomy U_Γ=(-1)^N；进一步以条件态描述量子源与场，说明反作用可引入附加相位并降低干涉可见度，其条件态重叠为中心标量–伪标量元素，表明仅保持fluxoid扇区并不保证单位相干性。

Conclusion: 几何代数（Cl(3,0)）可将磁AB效应的相位、通量、符号与超导fluxoid量子化统一在单一内蕴框架内；理想极限下电子holonomy呈二值(-1)^N。但fluxoid扇区的保持不等价于相干性保持：反作用导致的附加相位会削弱干涉可见度，须以中心标量–伪标量重叠元评估实际相干性。

Abstract: The magnetic Aharonov--Bohm (AB) effect is often introduced through the idealised infinite solenoid and a complex scalar phase. We give a geometric-algebra formulation based on the compact toroidal magnets shielded by a superconductor employed in the electron-holography experiments of Tonomura and collaborators. Working in the real Euclidean Clifford algebra $ \mathrm{Cl}(3,0)$, we distinguish the unit volume pseudoscalar $I$ (grade 3), coordinate angles (scalars, grade 0), tangent vectors (grade 1), and directed surface bivectors (grade 2). The magnetic field is represented by the dual bivector $\mathcal{B}= I \mathbf B$, while the electromagnetic $U(1)$ phase is represented by the central unit multivector $e^{I\alpha}$ generated by the pseudoscalar $I$, rather than by a spatial rotor with a bivector generator. We establish the linked toroidal geometry, formulate magnetic flux and Stokes' theorem intrinsically in geometric algebra, and derive the holonomy element $U_\Gamma=\exp(Iq\Phi_B/\hbar)$ from $U(1)$ gauge covariance, with every sign fixed explicitly. Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are a hallmark of non-Hermitian physics, giving rise to extreme sensitivity and enhanced dynamic response. That is not necessarily the case in the quantum regime, where this kind of behavior around EPs, to a large extent, remains elusive. In fact, the interplay between dissipation and quantum fluctuations makes the complete Lindbladian evolution quite different from effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian descriptions. Conclusion: 改进的界可直接加强基于BB84 MoE博弈的安全性归约，从而提升相关密码协议的安全界与可认证噪声容忍度。

Abstract: The BB84 monogamy-of-entanglement (MoE) game has numerous applications in quantum cryptography, including quantum key distribution, position verification, and unclonable encryption. Abstract: Task-oriented quantum sensing aims to extract decision-relevant information directly, without first reconstructing the full signal. We study this approach for mixture-threshold classification of transient NMR-like signals generated from molecular spin Hamiltonians, motivated by small-volume molecular sensing. Using analytical models and numerical simulations, we compare classical coil readout followed by matched-filter classification with quantum computational sensing (QCS), in which trainable controls interleaved with signal-driven sensor-qubit evolution map the transient directly to a decision score. Ensemble accuracy can rise rapidly for some waveform pairs, whereas single-shot correctness improves more gradually because one projective outcome must distinguish the classes. Repeated acquisitions improve both routes by averaging independent fluctuations, while disorder-aware training mitigates quasistatic inhomogeneity in the modeled sensor ensemble. QCS can outperform inductive readout under the modeled conditions, for example when coil transfer is weak or receiver noise limits coil performance. However, the simulated QCS protocols at lower effective sensor coupling require long sensing intervals, whereas short-time protocols require a much stronger effective drive at the sensor. Practical advantage therefore depends on jointly attainable source-signal strength, signal-to-sensor transfer, qubit coherence, and readout resources.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate single-photon scattering and routing in an asymmetric cavity-waveguide quantum electrodynamics system consisting of a Jaynes-Cummings cavity-emitter subsystem coupled to two one-dimensional waveguides. Using a real-space scattering approach, we derive an exact three-channel scattering matrix and analytical expressions for the channel-resolved scattering amplitudes. The scattering response is governed by the interplay of asymmetric cavity-waveguide coupling, atom-cavity detuning, coherent preparation of a single-photon superposition across multiple input channels, and intrinsic dissipation. Coupling asymmetry controls the relative weights of the cavity-mediated scattering pathways, whereas atom-cavity detuning modifies their spectral response. Coherent multichannel input states provide an additional phase-sensitive control of the output probabilities through interference. In particular, we identify an analytical condition, $J_2=2J_1$ and $\alpha_1=-\alpha_2$, under which the probability in the second output channel becomes independent of photon energy; for an equal-weight normalized input, this probability is 1/2. We further show how cavity and atomic dissipation reduce the probability remaining in the guided channels. These results provide a framework for resonance-engineered single-photon routing in asymmetric Jaynes-Cummings cavity-waveguide systems.

Abstract: Many quantum codes, including balanced-product, bivariate-bicycle and genon codes and the five-qubit code, are quotient codes: a larger code folded onto itself by identifying each check with its image under a deck transformation composed with a symmetry. We show that this is the lattice form of twisted compactification, in which a theory on $X\times F$ carries a symmetry background along $F$, and we use the correspondence in both directions. For monomial quotients of Pauli stabilizer codes, a transfer theorem gives the logical qudits, logical operators and distance bounds from those of the parent, with a Tate cohomology group counting the logical operators that are new. For higher-form and cup-product symmetries the twist is an entangling diagonal circuit; when a finite cover trivializes the background, the code is a dressed quotient, so abelian Pauli codes give non-abelian topological order such as $D(D_4)$. The dressings have Clifford level at most one less than the degree of the twist, and for cubic twists the code dimension is a count of solutions of quadratic equations in cup products, which certifies that the code is not a Pauli stabilizer code when it is not a power of two; on tori and slabs the distance is linear in the size. Twists also create logical gates: three toric codes on $S^3$ have no logical qubits, but on $\mathbb{RP}^3$ the same circuit is a logical $CCZ$. A quarter-turn twist of the 3D fermionic toric code gives codes of linear distance, from $[[768,2,9]]$ up, whose $p+ip$ pump is an explicit finite-time quasi-local evolution that preserves the code space exactly; using the spin-topological response formula of Barkeshli, Hsin and Kobayashi, we identify its logical action as $T$ up to Clifford factors. Finally, twisting abelian gauge theories reach only the quantum doubles $D(G)$ with $[G,G]$ central.

</details>

Abstract: Time crystals are phases of matter that spontaneously break time-translation symmetry. They have been ruled out in the ground and Gibbs states of isolated systems with only short-range interactions, yet they have been proposed and observed experimentally in nonequilibrium settings such as Floquet and open systems. However, existing proposals for time crystals in open quantum systems require an external supply of thermodynamic resources, such as work or energetic coherence. Whether coupling to an environment, without any such resource supply, can induce a time crystal absent in the isolated system remains open. Here, we answer this question systematically. We first construct an explicit model and prove that coupling to an environment can activate a time crystal absent in the isolated system, even without such resource supply. We consider Lindblad master equations with (i) time-independent Hamiltonian and jump operators, (ii) time-translation covariance, and (iii) detailed balance---conditions that hold in standard derivations unless thermodynamic resources are supplied externally. Our model satisfies these conditions, and the activation occurs in a stationary state of both the isolated and open systems. We give several implementations of this dynamics with a zero-temperature Gibbs environment, a time-independent, energy-conserving interaction, and only short-range Hamiltonians, one of which exactly reproduces the time-crystalline periodicity. We then prove a no-go theorem: under (i)--(iii), activation can arise only from an environment-induced Hamiltonian term, not from dissipation. Our results show that time crystals can be activated in open systems without external resource supply and identify the physical requirements on the system and environment for this activation through our general no-go theorem.

</details>

Abstract: In analogy to the Kramers-Henneberger transformation in non-relativistic quantum mechanics, we propose and investigate a spinor transformation for the Dirac equation which allows us to transform space-time dependent vector potentials $A(t,x)\boldsymbol{e}$_x away. In the mass-less case, we exactly arrive at the free Dirac equation, while for massive Dirac fields, the transformation results in a non-trivial mass matrix containing space-time dependent phases. Special emphasis is placed on the phenomenon of fermion pair creation in the external field $A(t,x)\boldsymbol{e}_x$ where the spinor transformation allows us to conceptually disentangle the different contributions to pair creation. It can also be used to reformulate the WKB approach and the Riccati equation (which can be advantageous in certain cases) and to obtain additional insight into the mechanism of dynamically assisted pair creation.

Abstract: Local response measurements track how perturbations spread in quantum simulators and nonequilibrium many-body systems. Locality bounds constrain the propagation speed, but do not determine whether the response can distinguish computational outcomes at fixed measurement precision as the distance increases. Here we construct time-independent dynamics with bounded nearest-neighbor interactions in which a fixed single-site detector responds to a remote bond phase after a time proportional to their separation. Two source-phase settings give the susceptibility with a distance-independent shot count at fixed accuracy and confidence. The leading correction vanishes when exchanging the two internal states of the excitation reverses the interaction. Varying the source and observation time makes all bond phases locally identifiable near the zero-phase reference setting. We also prove a conditional quantum advantage for distribution-free response prediction.

Abstract: Environmental magic can enable two channels with zero quantum capacity to transmit quantum information jointly. A half-erasure auxiliary activates this channel through the Smith--Yard mechanism. Abstract: Free fermions exhibit Eigenstate Typicality (ET) meaning that the reduced density matrix, $\rho$, of a small spatial subsystem of a {\sl single}, typical, excited energy eigenstate is gaussian with a modular hamiltonian, $h \equiv -\log \rho$, asymptotically proportional to the free fermion hamiltonian. In effect, the ET may be thought of as providing an independent route to establishing the Fermi-Dirac (FD) distribution for free fermions. We revisit this argument for fermions belonging to a superposition of degenerate eigenstates. Specifically, we show that the modular hamiltonian corresponding to a superposition of degenerate eigenstates in a 1-d free fermion lattice system is interacting, and therefore the reduced density matrix for the subsystem is not gaussian. Measurements of the single particle distribution of a small subsystem would then exhibit non-thermal deviations from the FD distribution. These features may be traced to a violation of Wick's theorem for the chosen excited state. We develop a perturbation theory for the modular hamiltonian corresponding to a weak mixture of degenerate states and study the modular hamiltonian (exactly) computationally. Finally, we speculate that initial conditions in a realistic fermi system evolving towards equilibrium might be encoded in the interaction terms of the modular hamiltonian and consequent correlations of fermions.

</details>

Abstract: Non-distinguishability of non-orthogonal quantum states is the essential feature of quantum mechanics. In particular, one cannot deterministically orthogonalize the set of quantum states, while probabilistic orthogonalization is possible, including unambiguous state discrimination and discrimination between the subsets. Here, we propose a method for error-free subsets discrimination with significantly increased success probability, which may reach one even for non-orthogonal and linearly dependent input states. This method may be applied in quantum cryptography: An alternative basis reconciliation procedure is described, which may provide unit sifting probability even for a large number of bases and for non-orthogonal states within each basis.
