Abstract: We undertake an information theoretic study of instrument compression problems (ICPs) in {multiple network} configurations, wherein the goal is to design and analyze protocols that simulate specific quantum classical (QC) correlation amidst distributed parties - some holding components of the quantum state and the rest holding classical registers - with minimum communication. We present a \textit{unified framework} of protocols based on likelihood instruments whose analysis yields new inner bounds in the one-shot and asymptotic regimes for the different network configurations we study. Our framework comprises of a common template for designing the instrument simulation protocol and analyzing its information theoretic performance. We also address a related problem of multi-party purity distillation wherein three distributed parties have to distil pure states efficiently via local operations and minimal communication. In regards to the tools, in addition to one-shot purity concentration protocol of \cite{202203arXiv_ChaPadSen} and one-shot quantum simultaneous decoder of Sen \cite{202102SAD_Sen}, we leverage Sen's fully smooth multi-partite covering \cite{202504arXiv_Sen} for all of our covering bounds.

</details>

Abstract: It is known that the normal space to an orbit of a compact group linear action meets each orbit. We show that this yields known normal forms for quantum states. We compute the gradient flow converging to the normal form and find it gives a Riccati type matrix-valued ODE.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱信号检测受量子投影噪声（QPN）、光子散粒噪声（PSN）与本征热噪声（ITN）共同限制，需借助差分结构与噪声相关性来提升信噪比并降低可检测功率门限。

Method: 构建相位平衡差分Rydberg原子量子接收机，通过本振相位控制强化差分信号响应；采用共享压缩库模型关联单元间QPN与PSN；建立含QPN、PSN、ITN及单元间相关性的解析模型，推导方型M-QAM的SNR增益、中断约束最小可检测功率与BER，并用Monte-Carlo仿真（每点150,000次）验证。

Result: 当PSN相关系数趋近1时SNR增益约10 dB，BER随相关性增大而下降；Rician衰落、门限SNR为0 dB、最大中断概率0.1时，最小可检测功率由K=1.155的-146.95 dBm降至K=2.4的-159.98 dBm。解析结果与仿真吻合。

Conclusion: 差分结构结合噪声相关性利用可显著提升Rydberg量子接收机的弱信号检测性能，噪声相关性是SNR增益与误码性能的关键可控自由度。

Abstract: This paper proposes a phase-balanced differential Rydberg atomic quantum receiver (DRAQR) for weak-signal recovery, using local-oscillator phase control to reinforce differential signal responses and a shared squeezed-reservoir model to correlate inter-cell quantum projection noise (QPN) and photon shot noise (PSN). An analytical model incorporating QPN, PSN, intrinsic thermal noise (ITN), and inter-cell correlations quantifies signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) gain, outage-constrained minimum detectable power, and bit-error rate (BER) for square $M$-QAM. For the evaluated settings, SNR gain reaches approximately $10$~dB as PSN correlation approaches unity, with BER decreasing as correlation increases. Under Rician fading, at a $0$-dB threshold SNR and maximum outage probability of $0.1$, the minimum detectable power decreases from $-146.95$~dBm at $K=1.155$ to $-159.98$~dBm at $K=2.4$. Analytical results are validated against Monte-Carlo simulations over $150{,}000$ data runs per point for receiver-performance plots.

</details>


### [84] [Quantum Integration Software Suite For HPC-Quantum Computing Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09659)

TL;DR: 本文提出面向OpenQASM3.0的量子汇编编译器QASC，将量子程序编译为可执行文件并调用高性能模拟器QuEST进行模拟；同时提出性能建模工具QACC-perf，用于估计量子计算融入经典HPC工作流的集成代价与量子线路执行时间，并以ChemShell多尺度量子化学工作流验证其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高性能计算（HPC）与量子计算（QC）混合集成时缺乏有效的建模手段：如何编译量子程序、如何在HPC环境中高效模拟，以及如何量化QC接入经典工作流的代价与线路执行时间，均存在挑战。

Method: 1) 开发Quantum Assembly Compiler（QASC），解析OpenQASM3.0文件并生成可执行程序，借助高性能模拟器QuEST完成量子程序模拟；2) 开发性能建模工具QACC-perf，对量子计算集成进经典HPC工作流的成本及量子线路执行时间进行估算；3) 在ChemShell计算化学环境下构建多尺度量子化学工作流作为应用场景进行验证。

Result: QASC可实现OpenQASM3.0程序的编译与基于QuEST的高性能模拟执行；QACC-perf可给出QC-HPC集成代价与线路执行时间的估计；两者在ChemShell多尺度量子化学工作流中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 面向QC与HPC融合的编译与性能建模工具链（QASC与QACC-perf）可行且有效，可为量子-经典混合工作流的设计与性能评估提供支撑，并已在真实的多尺度量子化学应用中初步验证。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on addressing challenges related to modelling the integration of conventional high-performance computing (HPC) and quantum computing (QC). We introduce the Quantum Assembly Compiler (QASC) for OpenQASM3.0 files. The compiler produces an executable that leverages the high-performance simulator QuEST to simulate quantum programs. Additionally, we introduce a performance modelling tool, QACC-perf, which can estimate the cost of integrating QC in classical HPC workflows, as well as the execution time of the quantum circuits. Finally, we demonstrate the utility of these tools in the context of multiscale quantum chemistry workflows using the ChemShell computational chemistry environment.

</details>


### [85] [An Adaptive Quantum Search Framework under Unknown Solution-density: Minimizing Expected Queries to the First Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09688)
*Wiwat Asawalertsak,Rajchawit Sarochawikasit*

TL;DR: 本文针对Grover算法需预先已知解数量的前提，系统比较并计算优化BBHT、定点搜索和密度估计三类框架，并提出融合爬山辅助Grover自适应搜索与Wang-Landau密度估计的混合量子-经典自适应框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法依赖目标解数量以确定迭代次数，虽已有多种规避或识别该参数的方法，但缺乏直接的横向比较与统一优化基准。

Method: 在两种初始化代价约定下，针对不同解密度建立基准，计算优化BBHT、Fixed-Point与Density Estimation三类框架及其配置，选取最小期望首次成功查询数；进而构建融合爬山辅助Grover自适应搜索与Wang-Landau状态空间密度估计的混合量子-经典自适应框架，并辅以解析建模。

Result: 理想仿真下，所提框架的平均累计查询代价相比测试的随机化与定点基线降低18.8%–63.4%，解析模型与仿真结果相互印证。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了解数量未知情形下Grover类算法的统一比较与优化基准，并展示了密度估计驱动的混合自适应框架在查询代价上的实用优势。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm provides a mathematically optimal quadratic speedup for unstructured search, yet its standard formulation relies on a critical prerequisite: prior knowledge of the target solution count to determine the number of algorithm iterations. Although numerous strategies have been developed to identify or bypass this parameter, comparing them directly is nontrivial. This study investigates and computationally optimizes 3 prominent frameworks: Boyer-Brassard-Hoyer-Tapp, Fixed-Point search, and Density Estimation Methods. Density-specific benchmarks select the best tested method and configuration to minimize expected first-solution queries under 2 initialization-cost conventions. To translate these theoretical benchmarks into practical utility, a hybrid quantum-classical adaptive framework integrating Hill-climbing-assisted Grover Adaptive Search with state-space density estimation modeled via the Wang-Landau algorithm is proposed as a potential use-case. This implementation operates directly on the optimized query methods established earlier. Analytical models support the approach; ideal simulations show 18.8%-63.4% lower mean accumulated query costs than the tested randomized and fixed-point baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Shaping Frequency-Bin Biphoton Correlations via Programmable Optical Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09713)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作实现了一种无模型、硬件在环的光子对联合谱强度（JSI）整形方法：利用可编程光学频率梳驱动级联光子对源，并用分块同时扰动随机逼近（SPSA）控制器在3×3、4×4、5×5频率格点上优化多达22个泵浦/信号/闲频参数，每次迭代仅需2次硬件评估，即可收敛到多种目标图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率格量子信息处理需要对光子对谱关联进行可编程整形，但在缺乏可靠前向模型的非线性光学系统中，这种控制难以实现；因此需要一种不依赖精确端到端模型的直接优化手段。

Method: 采用可编程光学频率梳驱动级联光子对源，以实测联合谱强度为反馈，使用分块SPSA控制器同时优化泵浦、信号与闲频共至多22个参数；每次迭代只需2次被接受的硬件评估，且评估次数与参数数无关，从而在3×3、4×4、5×5频率格上逼近多样目标图样。

Result: 优化分布与目标的Pearson相关系数为0.66–0.98，经典重叠保真度为0.32–0.62；跨目标相似性分析表明优化图样具有目标特异性；源表征给出最大符合-偶然比约585。精度受泵浦相位传递、寄生非线性背景、波整形器约束、纯幅度滤波与测量噪声限制。

Conclusion: JSI不含谱相位信息，故结果仅证明对实测频率格布居数与关联的控制，而非双光子态的完整重构；需相位敏感表征以验证格间相干性与纠缠维度。该工作确立了在缺乏精确模型时，以直接测量反馈实现可重构频率格关联工程的实用途径。

Abstract: Programmable shaping of photon-pair spectral correlations is important for frequency-bin quantum information processing. However, achieving this control remains challenging in nonlinear optical systems without reliable forward models. Here, we demonstrate model-free, hardware-in-the-loop shaping of the measured joint spectral intensity of a cascaded photon-pair source driven by a programmable optical frequency comb. A blockwise simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation controller optimizes up to 22 pump, signal, and idler parameters. Each iteration requires only two accepted hardware evaluations, independent of the parameter count. The controller converges to diverse target patterns on 3$\times$3, 4$\times$4, and 5$\times$5 frequency-bin grids. The optimized distributions achieve Pearson correlations between 0.66 and 0.98 and classical overlap fidelities between 0.32 and 0.62. Cross-target similarity analysis confirms that the optimized patterns remain target-specific. Source characterization yields a maximum coincidence-to-accidental ratio of approximately 585. Accuracy is limited by pump-phase transfer, parasitic nonlinear background, waveshaper constraints, amplitude-only filtering, and measurement noise. Joint spectral intensity contains no spectral-phase information. Therefore, the results demonstrate control of measured frequency-bin populations and correlations, not complete reconstruction of the two-photon state. Phase-sensitive characterization is required to verify inter-bin coherence and quantify the entanglement dimensionality of the output state. Our results establish direct measurement feedback as a practical approach to reconfigurable frequency-bin correlation engineering when an accurate end-to-end model is unavailable.

</details>


### [87] [Witnessing Polarization Entanglement of Annihilation Photons via Compton Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09738)
*Tobias C. Sutter,Beatrix C. Hiesmayr*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将康普顿散射重构为连续结果量子仪器，把康普顿偏振测量与纠缠目击者联系起来，给出任意双光子偏振态的Bell态保真度下界，并可从粗粒化符合测量中提取，为仲正电子素衰变中的纠缠验证提供可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能区缺乏偏振器，湮灭光子的偏振纠缠验证只能依赖康普顿偏振测量；已有方案要么实验上不可行，要么依赖特定的源态模型。

Method: 把康普顿散射形式化为连续结果（continuous-outcome）量子仪器，从而可同时预测散射角与散射后偏振态；据此推导对任意初始双光子偏振态、实验可获取的Bell态保真度下界，构成纠缠目击者，并给出从任意角度分辨率探测器的粗粒化符合计数中提取该目击者的方法。

Result: 所得目击者不依赖与角度无关的经验因子（如探测器效率、源活度），且对探测器角分辨率要求宽松，可适用于粗粒化符合测量。

Conclusion: 该框架为现有技术条件下在仲正电子素衰变中实验验证偏振纠缠提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Experimentally certifying polarization entanglement in annihilation photons is challenging because polarizers are unavailable in the high-energy regime and one needs to rely on Compton polarimetry. Previously proposed schemes designed for this task are either experimentally unfeasible or require a specific source state model. We address these issues by reformulating Compton scattering as a continuous-outcome quantum instrument, thereby enabling the prediction of the scattering angles and the post-scattering polarization state. Our framework allows connecting Compton polarimetry with entanglement witnesses by deriving experimentally accessible lower bounds on Bell state fidelities for arbitrary initial two-photon polarization states. We discuss how this witness can be extracted from coarse-grained coincidence measurements with detectors of almost any angular resolution. The presented scheme does not require knowledge of angle-independent empirical factors like detector efficiencies and source activity. Thus, our results provide a feasible route towards experimental entanglement certification in para-positronium decay with current technology.

</details>


### [88] [Universal photonic synthetic temporal-mode converter for heterogeneous quantum interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09752)
*Joohyung Song,James I. Kwon,Edo Waks,Saikat Guha,Chaohan Cui*

TL;DR: 提出合成时间模式转换器（STC），将波形匹配与稠密时分复用结合，把N个时间bin的已知单光子波形相干汇聚为单一公共bin模式，实现异构量子互连中的高效模式转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构量子互连中不同光源产生的单光子存在时域波形不匹配与时间bin占用过宽的问题，导致模式重叠度低、光子对符合计数率受限，需要既能提升时间不可区分性又能释放信道时间资源的模式转换方案。

Method: STC由可编程耦合器与光延迟构成，利用时间bin间的相干干涉实现波形匹配；通过⌈log₂N⌉级混合级联，将N个bin的幅度集中到单一公共bin模式，等效于对已知单光子时间波形做幺正压缩变换。

Result: 仿真中指数型与高斯型输入的模式重叠均超过99.9%；N=256、每级0.5 dB损耗、两路指数型输入时，STC在相同模式重叠下光子对符合计数率较时间门控高45.6倍；相对长脉冲，在充分利用时分复用容量时可支持约41倍更高的符合计数率。

Conclusion: STC以对数级混合深度同时改善时间不可区分性与信道时间利用率，为异构量子互连中的高效模式匹配提供可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a synthetic temporal-mode converter (STC) that combines programmable waveform matching with denser time multiplexing for heterogeneous quantum interconnects. Programmable couplers and optical delays reshape known single-photon temporal waveforms through coherent interference between time bins. Conversion to a common single-bin mode concentrates amplitudes from $N$ bins in $\lceil\log_2N\rceil$ mixing stages, simultaneously improving temporal indistinguishability and freeing channel time. Simulations yield mode overlaps above 99.9\% for exponential and Gaussian inputs. For $N=256$ and $0.5\,\mathrm{dB}$ loss per stage, STC gives a 45.6-fold higher photon-pair coincidence rate than temporal gating at equal mode overlap for two exponential inputs. Relative to long pulses, the shortened temporal matched mode can support an approximately 41-fold higher photon-pair coincidence rate when the available time-multiplexing capacity is fully utilized.

</details>


### [89] [Scalable Unitary State-Encoding for Generic Large Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09760)
*Vittorio Pagni,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Michael Felderer,Michael Epping,Kevin Lively*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出无需振幅放大和中间测量的确定性可扩展振幅编码方法，通过将多控复用器电路简化为单/双量子比特门（布尔逻辑最小化与Walsh-Hadamard谱分解两条互补算法），在等保真度下较Qiskit默认方法将电路深度最多降低数个数量级，并在方形格子连接的IQM硬件上验证了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数据高效编码进量子态是许多量子计算协议的基本瓶颈，现有态制备方法门深度大，使应用在近中期硬件上尚未开始计算就已被噪声淹没，亟需低双比特门开销、深度与保真度可调的确定性编码方案。

Method: 提出两种互补算法简化全控复用器电路：(1) 将冗余控制位消除形式化映射为布尔逻辑最小化问题；(2) 借助单个辅助量子比特，利用门角度的Walsh-Hadamard谱将多控旋转门分解为单/双量子比特操作序列；并利用张量网络处理超出经典内存的数据或可查询函数，支持复杂数据编码，且方法可杂交使用。

Result: 在多种函数类上基准测试，刻画全连接图下总深度随系统规模与目标保真度的变化；与Qiskit默认态制备方法相比，混合方法在同等态保真度下电路深度最多改善数个数量级；在方形格子连接的IQM硬件上，即使采用极少的噪声缓解也获得优异保真度。

Conclusion: 通过显著降低编码门深度，本工作使原本在首次计算前即被噪声破坏的应用得以在当前及近中期硬件上实验执行，缩短了实现稳健量子实用性的时间尺度。

Abstract: Efficient encoding of data into quantum states remains a fundamental bottleneck in many quantum computing protocols, which would otherwise offer advantages over their classical counterparts. Here, we introduce deterministic and scalable amplitude-encoding methods with low two-qubit gate costs and tunable trade-offs between circuit depth and encoding fidelity. Our approaches require no amplitude amplification or mid-circuit measurement and are capable of encoding arbitrary complex data or queryable functions by using tensor-networks to go beyond what can be held in classical memory. Our principle contributions are two complementary algorithms for simplifying fully controlled multiplexer circuits to one- and two-qubit gates. In the first, we formally map the elimination of redundant gate controls to a Boolean logic minimization problem, while in the second we utilize a single ancilla qubit to map the multi-controlled rotation gates to a sequence of one- and two-qubit operations via the Walsh-Hadamard spectrum of the gate angles. We benchmark these methods across a broad range of function classes, characterizing their total depth in an all-to-all connectivity graph against system size and target fidelity. Hybridizing our methods gives up to orders of magnitude improvement in circuit depth at equivalent state fidelity compared to the default state-preparation method in the Qiskit package. We furthermore demonstrate our method on IQM hardware with a square lattice connectivity, finding excellent fidelity, even with minimal noise mitigation. By significantly reducing gate depth for encoding, this work enables the experimental execution of applications on current and near-term hardware which would otherwise be reduced to noise before the first calculation step, shortening the time horizon to robust quantum utility.

</details>


### [90] [Differentiable Digital Twins for Optimization, Calibration, and Characterization of Neutral-Atom- and Ion-based Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09779)
*Marco Rossignolo,Lorena C. Bianchet,Anurag Saha Roy,Shai Machnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一套用于高精度建模原子量子系统并模拟量子比特门动力学的软件工具集，将量子最优控制、基准测试与自动微分结合，实现基于梯度的高效优化，在中性原子与离子阱案例中提升了门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于单原子操控的量子技术具备良好相干性与可控性，是可扩展量子计算的有力平台；但系统对噪声和硬件不完美敏感，可靠的高保真门操作仍难以实现，缺乏高精度建模与门动力学仿真的集成工具。

Method: 构建综合性软件工具集：高精度建模原子量子系统、仿真量子比特门动力学；集成量子最优控制与基准测试方法，并引入自动微分以支持高效、准确的梯度优化。

Result: 通过涉及中性原子与囚禁离子的详细案例研究验证工具能力，显示其在提升量子门保真度方面的有效性，并为实验实现提供有价值的见解。

Conclusion: 该工具集可作为原子量子系统门设计与优化的实用平台，借助自动微分加速的最优控制提升门性能，并可衔接实验实现。

Abstract: Quantum technologies based on individually controlled atoms are among the most promising platforms for scalable quantum computing, owing to their favorable coherence properties and controllability. Nonetheless, achieving reliable and high-fidelity gate operations remains challenging due to the sensitivity of these systems to noise and hardware imperfections. In this work, we introduce a comprehensive software toolset designed to model atomic quantum systems with high precision and simulate qubit gate dynamics. By integrating quantum optimal control and benchmarking techniques with automatic differentiation for efficient and accurate gradient-based optimization, the toolset enables significant improvements in gate performance. We demonstrate its capabilities through detailed case studies involving both neutral atoms and trapped ions, highlighting its effectiveness in enhancing quantum gate fidelities, and providing valuable insights for experimental implementation.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond the $n/2$ Threshold: Quantum Query Complexity for Computing Hamming Weight and Symmetric Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09809)
*Yuan Deng,Zihan Hao,Honghao Lin,Qipeng Liu,Vahab Mirrokni,Jianan Shao,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族对称布尔函数，其在固定常数错误下需 n/2+Ω(n^{1/3}) 次量子查询；同时给出用 n/2+O(n^{1/3}) 次量子查询恢复精确汉明重量的算法，从而确定对称函数最坏情形与汉明重量恢复的最优附加阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: van Dam 的 oracle-interrogation 算法可用 n/2+O(√n) 次查询计算任意 n 位布尔函数；而 parity 恰好可用 ⌈n/2⌉ 次查询精确计算。对于单比特布尔输出，除 n/2 之外是否必须存在附加开销（additive overhead）一直未解决，本文旨在确定该附加开销的最优阶。

Method: 下界方面，显式构造一族对称布尔函数，并证明在固定正数常数错误下需要 n/2+Ω(n^{1/3}) 次量子查询。上界方面，设计一个在常数错误下有界的情况下用 n/2+O(n^{1/3}) 次查询精确恢复汉明重量的量子算法。

Result: 得到匹配的上下界：对称布尔函数最坏情形查询复杂度为 n/2+Θ(n^{1/3})（在足够小的常数错误下），汉明重量精确恢复的查询复杂度同样为 n/2+Θ(n^{1/3})；且该算法对任意对称布尔函数在相同查询界内可计算。

Conclusion: 对单比特输出而言，n/2 之外的附加开销是必需的，其最优阶为 Θ(n^{1/3})；该结论同时确定了汉明重量恢复与对称布尔函数最坏情形在足够小常数错误下的最优附加阶。

Abstract: The oracle-interrogation algorithm [van Dam, FOCS '98] computes every $n$-bit Boolean function with bounded error using $n/2+O(\sqrt n)$ quantum queries. In contrast, parity can be computed exactly using $\lceil n/2\rceil$ queries, but whether an additive overhead beyond $n/2$ is necessary for a single Boolean output has remained open. In this work, we construct an explicit family of symmetric Boolean functions requiring $n/2+\Omega(n^{1/3})$ quantum queries at a fixed positive constant error. We also give an algorithm that recovers the exact Hamming weight using $n/2+O(n^{1/3})$ quantum queries with bounded error, and therefore computes every symmetric Boolean function within the same query bound. Together, these results determine the optimal additive order for Hamming weight recovery and for the worst case over symmetric Boolean functions at sufficiently small constant error.

</details>


### [92] [Arrival-time problem in the quantum information era - observation of a Zeno crossover in monitored arrival-time distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09814)
*Yousef Mafi,K. G. S. H. Gunawardana,Kim Pöyhönen,Teemu Ojanen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究周期监测下自旋链与量子线路中的量子到达时间问题，发现存在量子Zeno区与弱监测区，两区由单粒子带宽决定的最优监测周期分隔；并在超导量子硬件上首次实验测量了单粒子与多粒子在一维、二维电路中的到达时间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子粒子的时间分布（到达时间）自量子力学诞生以来一直存在争议，缺乏实验测量，尤其在有监测、有相互作用的量子输运场景中尚属空白。

Method: 理论上分析周期监测自旋链与量子线路中的到达动力学，界定量子Zeno区与弱监测区的转变点；实验上利用超导量子硬件，测量一维与二维线路中单粒子及多个相互作用粒子的到达时间分布，并与量子轨迹理论对比。

Result: 测得的到达时间分布清晰分辨出两个区域，且与量子轨迹理论高度吻合；在相互作用多体系统中转变点依然保持，但多体干涉重塑了分布形态；二维几何下Zeno抑制效应同样存在。

Conclusion: 两种区域是受监测量子传播的普适特征而非单粒子效应；该工作为受监测量子输运中到达时间分布的首次实验测量，回应了量子力学中长期存在的难题之一。

Abstract: When does a quantum particle arrive at a detector? The temporal distributions of itinerant quantum particles have been debated since the birth of quantum mechanics. Here we study the arrival-time problem in periodically monitored spin chains and quantum circuits. The arrival dynamics exhibits two regimes, the quantum Zeno and the low-monitoring regime, separated by an optimal monitoring period set by the single-particle bandwidth. In support of the theory, on superconducting quantum hardware, we measure arrival-time distributions for single and multiple interacting particles in one- and two-dimensional circuits. The measured distributions resolve both regimes and agree closely with quantum-trajectory theory. The crossover persists in interacting many-particle systems: the optimal monitoring point is preserved while many-body interference reshapes the distributions. Zeno suppression likewise persists in two-dimensional geometry. Together, these results show that the two regimes are a generic feature of monitored propagation rather than a single-particle effect. To our knowledge, our work constitutes the first experimental measurement of arrival-time distributions in a monitored quantum transport setting, addressing one of the longest-standing problems in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [93] [Discovering formally verified quantum programs with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09822)
*Sergey Shirobokov,Ben Jaderberg,Katerina Hristova,George Tsoukalas,Johannes Bausch,Eric Wieser,Francisco J. H. Heras,Nicholas C. Rubin,Guang Hao Low,Sébastien Racanière,Nenad Tomašev,Lei M. Zhang,James D. Watson,Tanuj Khattar,Aws Albarghouthi,Stavan Jain,Harun Khan,Leopoldo Sarra,Andrew W. Senior,Michael Broughton,Hussain Masoom,Sergio Boixo,Ryan Babbush,Swarat Chaudhuri,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于 Lean 定理证明器的量子程序库，使 Gemini 4 智能体在形式化规约下自动合成并验证量子程序，在并行搜索、量子算术及聚变、光化学、药物代谢模拟等任务上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算能否实现科学突破，既依赖硬件进步，也依赖量子算法的改进；而算法突破通常需要多年专家协作。数学与计算机科学领域的大模型已通过自动化发现循环加速进展，但量子算法难以被经典计算机在一般规模上评估，因而缺乏可靠的自动化发现范式。

Method: 在 Lean 定理证明器中建立量子程序库，由 Gemini 4 智能体依据形式化问题规约合成新程序，并用机器验证其正确性与代价；智能体以团队形式协作，通过 wiki 共享结果与知识，并在组织的"会议"中讨论想法。

Result: 在多项基线上取得改进：并行搜索最高 2.55 倍、量子算术 290 倍，以及与聚变、光化学、药物代谢相关的模拟分别加速 11 倍、8 倍和 2.5 倍。

Conclusion: 在数学证明的严谨性保障下大规模结合人类与人工智能，可设计出当今经典与量子计算机均无法直接测试的算法，从而缩短通往实用量子计算的路径。

Abstract: The timeline by which quantum computers can solve breakthrough problems in science depends on improved quantum algorithms as much as on better hardware. Such algorithmic advances are typically achieved through many years of expert human collaboration. In mathematics and computer science, large language models have accelerated progress through automated discovery loops. However, quantum algorithms can only be evaluated by classical computers on restricted or small instances. Here we demonstrate automated discovery of quantum programs beyond these regimes including order-of-magnitude improvements on scientific applications. To achieve this, we build a quantum program library in the Lean theorem prover in which Gemini 4 agents synthesize novel programs against formal problem specifications and verify their correctness and cost. Agent teams collaborate, sharing results and knowledge through wikis and discussing ideas at organized conferences. Our results improve on prior baselines for parallel search (up to 2.55x), quantum arithmetic (290x), and simulations relevant to fusion, photochemistry and drug-metabolism by 11x, 8x and 2.5x respectively. Combining human and artificial intelligence at scale, under the rigor of mathematical proof, lets researchers design algorithms that today's classical and quantum computers cannot test, shortening the path to practical quantum computation.

</details>


### [94] [Deterministic Quantum Search for NP Problems Through Verifier-Based Oracle Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09830)
*Soumyajyoti Chatterjee,Harishankar Mishra,Asvija Balasubramanyam,S D Sudarsan,Gudapati Naresh Raghava*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向NP问题的通用量子oracle构造方法与确定性量子搜索框架，直接由经典验证函数构建，保持O(√N)查询加速与多项式门开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典穷举求解NP问题最坏需O(N)次验证器查询；Grover类量子搜索虽可降至O(√N)，但通常假设oracle可直接识别解，且标准实现只给出概率性成功，缺乏对NP问题通用、可构造、确定性的方案。

Method: 基于NP问题的经典验证函数构造通用量子oracle，并设计确定性量子搜索框架，利用验证函数识别满足性赋值；分析oracle的门复杂度与量子比特需求。

Result: 框架保持相对经典穷举的二次查询加速；所构造oracle可用关于验证函数二进制编码长度L的多项式数量门实现，总量子比特数为O(poly(L))，与经典验证器多项式时间可计算性一致；在多个N-SAT实例上的电路级仿真确认了满足性赋值的确定性识别。

Conclusion: 确定性量子搜索可应用于一般NP问题，同时保留二次查询优势；为验证器驱动的量子求解提供了可构造、资源多项式可控的实用路径。

Abstract: Problems in the class NP are important in computational science, where a given candidate solution can be verified in polynomial time, while finding a solution may require evaluating the entire search space in the worst case. Classical exhaustive approaches generally solve such problems by evaluating the verifier function over all possible inputs, resulting in a worst-case query complexity of O(N), where N = d^n represents the size of the search space, d is the number of possible values for each variable, and n is the number of variables. Quantum search algorithms can solve unstructured search problems more efficiently by reducing the query complexity to O(sqrt (N)), providing a quadratic speedup over classical exhaustive search. However, conventional quantum search formulations typically assume access to an oracle that identifies valid solutions, while standard implementations generally yield probabilistic success. In this work, we propose a generalised quantum oracle construction method for NP problems, together with a deterministic quantum search framework that identifies satisfying assignments using their corresponding classical verifier functions. The proposed framework retains the quadratic query speedup of quantum search over classical exhaustive search. We further establish that constructed oracle can be implemented using a number of gates polynomial in the size (L) of the binary encoding of verification function, consistent with the polynomial-time evaluation of the corresponding classical verifier, while the total qubit requirement remains O(poly(L)). The construction is demonstrated on multiple N-SAT instances, with circuit-level simulations confirming deterministic identification of satisfying assignments. These results establish the applicability of deterministic quantum search to NP problems while retaining the quadratic query advantage over classical exhaustive search.

</details>


### [95] [Gibbsian Entropy for General Wigner Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09839)
*Gabriel Rabelo-Soares,Yevgeniy Kovchegov,Giorgio Torrieri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可从Wigner函数计算的非平衡Gibbs熵，适用于高斯与非高斯量子态，并证明大数定律、中心极限定理及熵与累积量生成函数的关系在准概率框架下仍成立，由此借助大偏差理论给出正定熵定义，同时导出相对熵、条件熵、互信息与Rényi熵，并与一维谐振子的Wehrl熵作比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs熵主要适用于平衡态，而量子系统在非平衡情形下缺乏从相空间准概率分布出发、且保持正定性与信息论意义的熵定义，因此需要构造可推广到非平衡 regime 的Gibbsian熵。

Method: 以Wigner函数为出发点，论证尽管其为准概率分布，但重复观测下联系可观测量的基本结论（大数定律、中心极限定理、熵与累积量生成函数的关系）依然有效；以大偏差理论为核心，基于对重复测量累积量后底层态结构的"无知"构造正定熵定义，并推导相对熵、条件熵、互信息和Rényi熵等信息论量。

Result: 得到与Gibbs熵形式平行、可用于非平衡情形的正定熵定义并讨论其重要性质；在一维谐振子的纯态与混合态上，将新熵与Wehrl熵进行数值对比。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架可推广至超越纠缠的量子关联研究以及封闭量子系统中的热化问题等潜在应用。

Abstract: We propose a definition of non-equilibrium Gibbsian entropy applicable to general quantum systems, computable from Wigner functions, for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states. We show that while the Wigner function is a quasi-probability distribution, fundamental results linking observables in repeated observations to the function, such as the law of large numbers, the central limit theorem and, crucially, the relation between the entropy and the cumulant generating function continue to apply. This ideas are the core of the large deviation theory, which allow to formulate a positive-definite definition of entropy based on our ignorance of the underlying state structure after repeated measurements of cumulants. This new definition is a parallel to the Gibbs entropy formulation, but it can also be used in out-of-equilibrium regimes. Some important properties of this entropy are discussed. In addition we derive some information theory quantities such as relative entropy, conditional entropy, mutual information and Rényi entropy. We then compare the results with Wehrl entropy for the 1D harmonic oscillator, for pure and mixed states. We conclude the the work discussing potential applications of this new formalism, ranging from quantum correlations beyond entanglement to thermalization in closed quantum systems.

</details>


### [96] [Novikov-Magnus Master Equation for Driven Quantum Systems under Classical Correlated Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09876)
*Nada Khogeer,Jerison Parra,Caroline Desgranges,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架，将Donsker-Furutsu-Novikov关系与Magnus展开结合，推导驱动量子系统在时间关联经典噪声下的Novikov-Magnus量子主方程，并验证其二阶与四阶映射的CPTP性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动量子系统受时间关联经典噪声影响时，需要时间局域的量子主方程；现有微扰方法难以同时保留噪声沿受控轨迹的非对易时序作用，且需在收敛性与CPTP性之间权衡。

Method: 以DFN泛函响应关系闭合噪声平均，用Magnus展开的嵌套对易子保留噪声诱导扰动的时间序作用；对零均值高斯噪声推导至噪声强度四阶的Novikov-Magnus主方程；并通过连通高阶噪声关联加权随机映射的高阶泛函导数，给出超越高斯噪声的自然推广。

Result: 在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck与带限1/f噪声下的驱动量子比特算例中，二阶与四阶映射在微扰区间内保持CPTP，并准确重现随机模拟；其金刚石范数误差与基于累积量的伪Lindblad主方程相当，Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声下四阶略有改进。

Conclusion: 该框架为含时间关联经典噪声的驱动量子系统提供了系统化、可扩展至非高斯噪声的时间局域主方程构造方案，兼顾非对易时序效应与CPTP性质。

Abstract: We introduce a modular framework for deriving time-local quantum master equations for driven systems subject to temporally correlated classical noise. The construction combines the Donsker-Furutsu-Novikov relation with the Magnus expansion: Functional response closes the noise average, while nested commutators retain the noncommuting chronological action of noise-induced perturbations along the controlled quantum trajectory. For zero-mean Gaussian noise, we derive the resulting Novikov-Magnus quantum master equation through fourth order in the noise strength. The same response hierarchy suggests a natural extension beyond Gaussian noise, with connected higher-order noise correlations weighting higher functional derivatives of the stochastic map. For a driven qubit under Ornstein-Uhlenbeck and band-limited $1/f$ noise, the second- and fourth-order maps remain CPTP throughout the perturbative regime and accurately reproduce stochastic simulations. Their diamond-norm errors are comparable to those of the cumulant-based pseudo-Lindblad master equation, with a modest fourth-order improvement for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise.

</details>

### [97] [Thermodynamic Phase Diagrams in $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaBr$_x$Cl$_{4-x}$ under Physical and Chemical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09320)
*Aulia Anisa Firdaus,Arifa Diana Agustin,Fathan Muyassar Santana,Taiyo Oka,Shuhei Fukuoka,Atsushi Kawamoto,Dita Puspita Sari,Yasuyuki Ishii*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过磁性与输运测量研究混晶系列λ-(BETS)₂GaBr_xCl_{4-x}超导态中电子关联的演化，结合化学压力与物理压力数据构建了带宽控制的压力-温度相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究化学压力（Br取代Cl）与物理压力如何共同调控λ型分子超导体的电子关联与超导态，并检验能否用统一的带宽控制相图描述化学取代、外压与分子取代三种调控手段。

Method: 制备混晶λ-(BETS)₂GaBr_xCl_{4-x}单晶，用电子探针微分析（EPMA）与单晶X射线衍射精修确定Br含量；开展退磁校正的磁化率与磁化测量确定下临界场H_c1；首次在该体系进行高场磁阻测量获得上临界场H_c2；结合已报道的化学压力与物理压力输运数据构建相图。

Result: x=0.22、0.24、0.42、0.63样品均显示抗磁超导转变；H_c1在x=0.24处呈现穹顶状极大值；H_c2随Br含量增加至x=0.42单调降低，确立了化学压力侧的高场超导边界；在化学压力下也观察到此前仅在物理压力下报道的低温电阻上翘。

Conclusion: 提出λ-(BETS)₂GaCl₄的双穹顶状P-T相图，其局部极小位于x≈0附近，P≈0.13 GPa处存在量子临界点，分隔低压力区（超导与磁性共存、非规金属态）与高压力区（常规金属行为）；SDW涨落在化学压力与物理压力两个轴向上连续延伸；该相图与λ-(STF)₂GaCl₄物理压力相图重合，说明化学取代、外压与分子取代可统一于单一带宽控制相图。

Abstract: The evolution of electron correlation in the superconducting state of the mixed-crystal series $\lambda$-(BETS)$_2$GaBr$_x$Cl$_{4-x}$ was investigated by magnetic and transport measurements. Br concentrations evaluated by electron-probe microanalysis agree well with single-crystal X-ray diffraction refinement. Demagnetization-corrected susceptibility and magnetization of crystals with $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.22, 0.24, 0.42, and 0.63 all show a diamagnetic superconducting transition. The lower critical field $H_{c1}$, determined from the deviation from the Meissner response, shows a dome-like maximum at $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.24. The upper critical field $H_{c2}$, obtained from the first high-field magnetoresistance measurements in this system, decreases with increasing Br concentration up to $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.42, establishing the high-field superconducting boundary on the chemical-pressure side. Combining these results with reported transport data under chemical and physical pressure, we propose a pressure-temperature phase diagram for $\lambda$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$. Its double-dome-like structure has a local minimum near x = 0, where a quantum-critical point was proposed at P $\approx$ 0.13 GPa, separating a low-pressure regime of superconductivity coexisting with magnetism and an unconventional metallic state from a higher-pressure regime with conventional metallic behavior. A low-temperature resistivity upturn, previously reported only under physical pressure, is also found under chemical pressure, suggesting that spin-density-wave fluctuations may extend continuously across both pressure axes. The phase diagram overlaps with that of $\lambda$-(STF)$_2$GaCl$_4$ under physical pressure, indicating that chemical substitution, external pressure, and molecular substitution can be unified in a single bandwidth-controlled phase diagram for $\lambda$-type molecular superconductors.

</details>


### [98] [Energy- and momentum-resolved Cooper-pair emission from a cuprate superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09857)
*Henry Amir,Jack Zwettler,Ka Ho Wong,Genda Gu,Rafael M. Fernandes,Jorge Noronha,Dirk K. Morr,Fahad Mahmood*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用双电子角分辨光电子能谱（2e-ARPES）在最优掺杂铜氧化物超导体中观测到库珀对发射：超导态下出现零对结合能、零对动量的双电子发射峰，正常态消失；且随温度降低，联合能量分布在相对能量轴显著展宽而总能宽度几乎不变，该反常行为不能用仪器分辨率、静态无序或热展宽解释，指向超导集体激发的散射过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导源于库珀配对，但常规光电子能谱只能逐个探测电子，无法直接观测配对电子对，因此需要能直接探测库珀对（对结合能与对动量）的实验手段。

Method: 采用双电子角分辨光电子能谱（2e-ARPES），对最优掺杂铜氧化物超导体进行符合测量，提取双电子发射峰，并在超导态与正常态之间比较；通过将联合能量分布分解为总能与相对能两个轴，考察温度依赖，并排除仪器分辨率、静态无序与热展宽等平凡解释。

Result: 在Tc以下观察到零对结合能、零对动量的双电子发射峰，正常态下消失，符合库珀对特征指纹；随温度降低，相对能量轴上的联合能量分布明显展宽，而总能宽度基本不变；该反常行为无法由仪器分辨率、静态无序或热展宽解释。

Conclusion: 2e-ARPES 可同时作为库珀对发射与超导态多体动力学的探针；相对能量展宽归因于涉及超导集体激发的散射过程，为研究配对与集体模式相互作用提供新途径。

Abstract: Superconductivity arises from Cooper pairing, but conventional photoemission probes electrons individually rather than as pairs. Here we report the observation of Cooper-pair emission from an optimally doped cuprate superconductor using two-electron angle-resolved photoemission (2e-ARPES). Below the superconducting transition, we observe a distinct two-electron emission peak at zero pair binding energy and zero pair momentum that disappears in the normal state, revealing the characteristic fingerprint of a Cooper pair. Unexpectedly, the joint energy distribution broadens markedly along the relative-energy axis as temperature decreases, while the total energy width remains nearly unchanged. This anomalous behavior cannot be explained by instrumental resolution, static disorder or thermal broadening, and instead points to scattering processes involving collective excitations of the superconductor. Our results establish 2e-ARPES as a probe of both Cooper-pair emission and the many-body dynamics of the superconducting state.

</details>

### [99] [Resolving non-neutrality across scales in a hyperdiverse tropical forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09479)
*Pietro Zgaga,Pablo Villegas,Tancredi Caruso,Tommaso Gili*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文借鉴统计力学，将随机矩阵滤波与空间显式中性模型结合，构建研究空间粗粒化下种间空间相关的框架，并在巴拿马BCI热带森林8次样地调查数据中识别出从"相互作用"到"有效中性"的连续渡越尺度（约20–35 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同生态过程作用于不同时空尺度，忽略微观细节的宏观描述可能捕捉到规律性。但"中性描述在何种尺度上成立"缺乏可操作的判定方法，需要一个能在空间粗粒化下追踪种间空间相关、并定位中性有效性阈值的框架。

Method: 构建受统计力学启发的框架：把随机矩阵滤波（用于提取种间相关谱、去除有限采样噪声）与空间显式中性模型相结合，对经验数据与中性预期在不同粗粒化尺度下进行逐级比较；应用于巴拿马Barro Colorado Island热带森林8次全样地清查数据。

Result: 观察到从相互作用型到有效中性组织的连续渡越。在约20–35 m以下，经验相关谱偏离中性预期，显示尺度依赖的物种特异性结构；其中部分可由环境异质性解释，残余空间关联则可作为生物相互作用或其他未测量的非中性过程的候选信号。随粗粒化增强，经验与中性相关谱逐步收敛，超过渡越尺度后中性模型能准确描述所测得的集体组织。

Conclusion: 中性可作为一种有效描述，在特征尺度之上刻画森林的集体组织，但这并不意味物种在个体层面等价；该框架提供了定位这一特征尺度的可操作方法。

Abstract: Different ecological processes operate at different spatial and temporal scales. Macroscopic regularities can therefore be captured by descriptions that ignore microscopic details, even when those details are mechanistically relevant to ecological processes. Here, we develop a framework inspired by statistical mechanics to track interspecific spatial correlations under spatial coarse-graining. To do so we combine random-matrix filtering with a spatially explicit neutral model. Applying this approach to eight censuses of the Barro Colorado Island tropical forest, we observe a continuous crossover from an interacting to an effectively neutral organization. Below a few tens of metres (20--35~m), the empirical correlation spectrum differs from neutral expectations, revealing scale-dependent species-specific structure. Part of this structure can be attributed to environmental heterogeneity. However, residual spatial associations provide candidate signatures of biotic interactions or other unmeasured ecological, non-neutral processes. With increasing spatial coarse-graining, empirical and neutral correlation spectra progressively converge, and above the crossover scale the neutral model provides an accurate description of the measured collective organization. Neutrality thus emerges as an effective description of the collective organization of the forest above a characteristic scale, without implying that species are equivalent at the individual level. Our framework provides an operational way to locate that scale.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.GA'></div>

### [100] [Violent relaxation redux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09531)
*Michael L. Nastac,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Robert J. Ewart,Michael Barnes,Alexander A. Schekochihin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该文提出无碰撞自引力系统（如并合星系）的剧烈弛豫确实产生Lynden-Bell最大熵平衡态，但机制在于引力湍流：大尺度引力涨落驱动相空间混沌混合，使相空间体积守恒（Casimir不变量）在对数于粒子数N的时间尺度上被破坏，系统因而沿一系列瞬时Lynden-Bell平衡态"绝热"演化，最终趋向最小能量、最大熵的"基态"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lynden-Bell的剧烈弛豫统计力学受相空间体积（无穷多无碰撞Casimir不变量）严格守恒约束，长期被认为难以解释无碰撞自引力系统的实际弛豫结果，形成"混沌多体系统趋于最大熵"这一统计力学原则的显眼例外。作者希望解决这一矛盾，说明剧烈弛豫为何以及如何仍能实现最大熵平衡。

Method: 提出引力湍流图像：大尺度引力涨落驱动分布函数的混沌相空间混合。解析推导该湍流的普适小尺度（亚Jeans尺度）能谱；估计相空间体积守恒被破坏的时间尺度，并与动力学（剧烈弛豫）时间比较；用一维N体数值模拟检验该图像。

Result: 得到湍流的普适亚Jeans尺度谱；证明相空间体积守恒的破坏时间尺度仅比动力学时间对数级地长（log N）；该时间尺度分离使系统能够"绝热"地经过一串Lynden-Bell平衡态序列，每个对应Casimir的瞬时取值；长时间后趋于接近"基态"的平衡，即Casimir已调整到使系统能量取最小可能值的分布。

Conclusion: 无碰撞自引力系统的普适倾向是最小能量、最大熵状态；一维N体模拟支持该图像，并推测可用于解释星系与暗物质晕的普适平衡态。

Abstract: Collisionless self-gravitating systems, such as merging galaxies, have long seemed an exception to the statistical-mechanical principle that chaotic many-body systems relax to maximum-entropy states: Lynden-Bell's statistical mechanics of their "violent relaxation," constrained by exact conservation of phase-space volume -- equivalently, of infinitely many collisionless "Casimir" invariants -- has appeared not to be a good description. We propose that violent relaxation does give rise to Lynden-Bell's maximum-entropy equilibria, but that both are underpinned by gravitational turbulence, in which large-scale gravitational fluctuations drive chaotic phase-space mixing of the particles' distribution function. We derive the universal small-scale (sub-Jeans) spectra of this turbulence and show that it leads to phase-space-volume conservation being broken on a timescale only logarithmically longer, in the particle number N, than the dynamical (violent-relaxation) time. This timescale separation lets the system relax "adiabatically" through a sequence of Lynden-Bell equilibria, each corresponding to instantaneous values of the Casimirs. At long times, it approaches an equilibrium close to its "ground state": a distribution whose Casimirs have adjusted so that the system's energy is now its minimum possible energy. Thus, the system's universal tendency is toward a minimum-energy, maximum-entropy state. We corroborate this picture with 1D N-body simulations and hypothesize that it can explain the universal equilibria of galaxies and dark-matter halos.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [pfoqcompiler: Efficient Compilation of Quantum Control in Recursive First-Order Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08503)
*Mário Silva,Alexandre Guernut,Marco Lewis*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出 pfoqcompiler：一个面向 Qiskit 的 FOQ 编译器，利用程序结构合并量子分支间兼容的递归过程调用，在保持既有复杂度刻画的前提下，对弱良基有界宽度程序生成多项式规模电路、对弱递归减半程序额外获得多对数深度，并以弱均匀性消除分支串行化的渐近开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归量子控制能简洁描述算法，但朴素电路编译会把 if 语句各分支串行化，导致电路深度指数增长；现有并行化技术虽可并行分支，却需指数级辅助量子比特。目标是在不付出指数辅助比特代价的情况下实现分支并行化。

Method: 设计并实现面向 Qiskit 的编译器 pfoqcompiler，针对一阶量子编程语言 FOQ；核心是借助程序结构，合并跨量子分支的、相互兼容的递归过程调用；同时给出更弱的可语法检查条件：弱良基性（weak well-foundedness）、弱递归减半（weak recursive halving）与弱均匀性（weak uniformity），并证明其保持先前的复杂度刻画。

Result: 对弱良基且有界宽度的程序，编译器生成多项式规模电路；若程序还满足弱递归减半，则额外达到多对数深度；弱均匀性进一步消除分支串行化带来的渐近开销。

Conclusion: 通过放宽语法可检查条件并利用程序结构合并递归调用，pfoqcompiler 在避免指数辅助比特的同时实现了递归量子控制的高效编译，将电路规模与深度的渐近界推广到更广的程序类。

Abstract: Recursive quantum control allows for simple and concise descriptions of algorithms. However, a naive circuit compilation sequentializes the different branches of the quantum if-statement and results in an exponential increase in circuit depth. Existing techniques can parallelize these branches, but do so at the cost of an exponential number of ancillary qubits. We present pfoqcompiler, a compiler targeting Qiskit for the first-order quantum programming language FOQ that exploits program structure to merge compatible recursive procedure calls across quantum branches. We introduce weaker syntactically-checkable conditions for well-foundedness, recursive halving, and uniformity, while preserving previously demonstrated complexity characterizations. For weakly well-founded bounded-width programs, the compiler produces polynomial-size circuits; weakly recursively halving programs additionally yield polylogarithmic depth, while weak uniformity removes the asymptotic overhead of branch sequentialization.

</details>


### [102] [A Complete Calculus for Hamiltonian Quantum Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08675)
*Jacques Carette,Chris Heunen,Robin Kaarsgaard,Neil J. Ross,Amr Sabry*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hamilton——一种并行算子为"同时作用的相互作用之和"的进程演算，其操作语义基于Lie-Trotter乘积公式，程序编译即按精度反复二分并把交错展开为量子线路。该演算具有完全抽象性、可表达哈密顿量恰为Pauli系数形如 m/2^k 者、且等式理论完备可判定，并配有完整实现与Agda形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算通过演化哈密顿量来模拟物理系统、求解优化问题与估计特征值，而硬件上连通的电路抽象最终也编译为哈密顿量。现有工作缺乏一个从高层并发式程序描述到哈密顿量、再到线路编译的统一形式化基础，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义进程演算 Hamilton，以并行算子表示同时作用的相互作用之和；用Lie-Trotter乘积公式作为操作语义，将哈密顿量之和恢复为越来越细的串行交错序列之极限；编译时将程序按选定精度反复二分，并把交错展开为量子线路。随后证明语义的完全抽象性、可表达哈密顿量的刻画（Pauli系数为 m/2^k）、等式理论的完备性与可判定性。实现层面给出完整编译器与等价性判定器，并用 Agda 对核心元理论进行机器检验。

Result: 语义完全抽象；可表达的哈密顿量恰为Pauli系数具有 m/2^k 形式者，这正是逐次二分所能达到的精度；等式理论完备且可判定。完整实现可将 Hamilton 程序编译为线路并判定其等价性，Agda 证明了核心元理论的正确性。

Conclusion: Hamilton 为"量子程序—哈密顿量—量子线路"提供了统一的演算基础，用 Lie-Trotter 公式把并发语义与编译精度自然联系起来，兼具理论上的完备性与可实现性，为量子程序的等价性验证与编译正确性提供了形式化工具。

Abstract: Given the forces acting on a quantum particle, a Hamiltonian models its continuous evolution. Quantum computers evolve Hamiltonians to simulate physical systems, to solve optimisation problems, and to estimate eigenvalues, and even the popular circuit abstraction is compiled to Hamiltonians on the hardware. We introduce Hamilton, a process calculus whose parallel operator is the sum of interactions acting simultaneously. Its operational semantics is the Lie-Trotter product formula, which recovers a sum as the limit of ever finer sequential interleavings; compiling a program means halving it to a chosen precision and unrolling the interleaving into a circuit. The semantics is fully abstract, the expressible Hamiltonians are exactly those whose Pauli coefficients have the form $m/2^k$, the precision that successive halving reaches, and the equational theory is complete and decidable. A full implementation compiles Hamilton programs to circuits and decides their equivalence, and Agda proofs sanity-check the core metatheory.

</details>

### [103] [All Symmetries and Boundaries of the Two-Dimensional Free Scalar from Continuous Flat-Gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40818)
*Yichul Choi,Ho Tat Lam,Brandon C. Rayhaun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出"远程可探测性判据"：二维CFT中任何超出左右移动Virasoro代数乘积的局域算符必可被某拓扑线缺陷探测；据此论证SU(2)_1 WZW模型的已知边界与拓扑线模空间是完备的，并通过U(1)"平坦规范化"完全分类自由标量场的酉共形边界与酉拓扑线缺陷，发现4维拓扑线缺陷模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由标量场/非紧致玻色子CFT中酉共形边界条件与酉拓扑线缺陷的完整分类长期不明确，已知结果零散且缺乏完备性判据；需要一个可操作的判据来保证分类不遗漏。

Method: 1) 提出远程可探测性判据（REMOTE DETECTABILITY）：非Virasoro×Virasoro代数内的局域算符均可由某拓扑线缺陷检测，并与共形网文献中的既有结论作对照；2) 以SU(2)_1 WZW为基准，用该判据论证SU(2)边界模空间与SO(4)拓扑线模空间完备；3) 通过对U(1)对称性做平坦规范化（flat-gauging）从SU(2)_1得到非紧致玻色子，从而转移分类结论；4) 写出所有边界与线的具体拉格朗日描述，计算边界态、区间Hilbert空间、环面配分函数验证开/闭对偶，并计算融合规则、线对局域算符的作用、平行融合对边界的作用及扭曲扇区Hilbert空间。

Result: 完全分类了二维自由标量场的酉共形边界条件与酉拓扑线缺陷；发现拓扑线缺陷具有4维模空间，其中大部分为此前未知；显式构造所有边界与线的拉格朗日描述，验证环面配分函数满足开/闭对偶，并给出融合规则、对局域算符的作用、平行融合作用及扭曲扇区Hilbert空间。

Conclusion: 远程可探测性判据可作为判定边界/缺陷分类完备性的有效工具；SU(2)_1与非紧致玻色子的酉共形边界与酉拓扑线缺陷由此得到完整分类，且自由标量场的拓扑线缺陷空间远大于先前认知（4维）。

Abstract: We propose a remote detectability criterion for two-dimensional conformal field theories, which asserts that any local operator outside of the product of the left- and right-moving Virasoro algebras can be detected by some topological line defect. We discuss its relationship to existing results in the conformal net literature. We then use this criterion to argue that the known $SU(2)$ moduli space of unitary conformal boundary conditions and the $SO(4)$ moduli space of unitary topological line defects of the $SU(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten model completely capture all unitary conformal boundaries and unitary topological lines of the theory. Invoking the fact that the non-compact boson conformal field theory can be obtained from $SU(2)_1$ by "flat-gauging" a $U(1)$ symmetry, we are also able to completely classify the unitary conformal boundaries and unitary topological line defects of the two-dimensional free scalar field. In particular, we find a 4-dimensional moduli space of topological line defects, most of which were not known previously. We support our proposed classifications by writing down concrete Lagrangian descriptions of all of the boundaries and lines of the non-compact boson. For every boundary condition, we calculate the corresponding boundary state, and for each pair of boundary conditions, we calculate the corresponding interval Hilbert space. We verify that the annulus partition functions of the theory respect open/closed duality. We also calculate the fusion rules of the topological line defects, their action on local operators, their action on the boundary conditions by parallel fusion, and their corresponding twisted-sector Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [104] [Andreev reflections and optimal truncation in the unitary Fermi droplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08580)
*Silas R. Beane,Adele Le Borgne,Domenico Orlando,Susanne Reffert,Maximilian Schwick*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究大味数N、大电荷Q（固定Q/N）双标度极限下外势阱中的幺正费米气体：领头阶化为非均匀配对背景下BdG算符的谱zeta函数，巨势梯度展开分体相与边缘两支；谐振阱下体相展开在第三项后截断（源于Son-Wingate EFT中Euler beta函数零点，与低能常数无关，并推广至任意奇数维各向异性阱），非谐振阱下体相渐近级数因子性发散，其大阶行为由BdG复周期轨道（Andreev反射的解析延拓）决定，并用四次、六次阱数值验证Borel奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双标度极限（N→∞、Q→∞、Q/N固定）下系统研究幺正费米气体在外势阱中的热力学结构，特别是梯度展开的体相—边缘分解及其收敛性质，并厘清非谐振阱下渐近级数的发散机制。

Method: 采用大N与大Q的双标度极限，将问题约化为非均匀配对背景中BdG算符的谱zeta函数；对巨势作梯度展开并区分体相与边缘贡献；借助Son-Wingate有效场论中Euler beta函数的零点分析体相项的抵消；对非谐振阱用热核WKB分析研究大阶发散，并识别复周期轨道（Andreev反射的解析延拓）作为Borel奇点来源；对四次与六次阱作数值验证。

Result: 谐振阱下体相展开在首三项之后截断，所有更高阶体相贡献积分后为零；该抵消具有普适性，不依赖低能常数，且在任意奇数维的各向异性阱中仍成立，故谐振势下所有高阶依赖均由液滴边缘承担。非谐振阱下体相渐近级数因子性发散，其大阶增长、符号振荡与最优截断由BdG复周期轨道固定；四次与六次阱的数值结果确认了预言的Borel奇点。

Conclusion: 梯度展开存在清晰的体相—边缘二分：谐振阱中体相被精确截断，高阶物理完全归于边缘自由度；非谐振阱中体相成为因子性发散的渐近级数，其非微扰结构由复周期轨道（Andreev反射的解析延拓）编码，从而把Borel求和奇点与半经典周期轨道图像联系起来。

Abstract: We study the unitary Fermi gas in an external trap in the double-scaling limit of large flavor number N and large charge Q at fixed Q/N. At leading order, the problem reduces to the spectral zeta function of a BdG operator in an inhomogeneous pairing background. The grand potential admits a gradient expansion with distinct bulk and edge sectors. We show that when the trap is harmonic, the bulk sector expansion terminates: beyond the first three terms every bulk contribution integrates to zero. This cancellation is general and follows from zeros of the Euler beta function computed in the Son-Wingate effective field theory, independent of the low-energy constants. The truncation persists for anisotropic traps in any odd dimension. All higher-order dependence for harmonic potentials is therefore carried by the droplet edge. For anharmonic traps, the asymptotic bulk series is factorially divergent. Using a heat-kernel WKB analysis, we show that its large-order growth, sign oscillations, and optimal truncation are fixed by complex periodic orbits of the BdG problem, which are analytic continuations of Andreev reflections. We numerically confirm the predicted Borel singularities for quartic and sextic traps.

</details>


### [105] [Analytic Bounds on Conformal Defect Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08582)
*Yuma Furuta,Wataru Harada,Yuya Kusuki,Yin Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用缺陷改变算符四点函数的共形自举，仅凭两个缺陷熵与一个缺陷改变算符的维数，导出缺陷上最低非真空标度维数的解析上界，并借此判断相关算符必然出现于熵较大的缺陷上；该结果适用于幺正共形线缺陷与二维CFT的共形边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷（杂质与边界）的稳定性取决于其上局域算符的标度维数，但这些维数通常难以确定，缺乏仅依赖少量易得数据即可判定相关算符存在的判据。

Method: 基于缺陷改变算符四点函数的共形自举（bootstrap），推导最低非真空标度维数的解析上界，输入仅为两个缺陷熵与一个缺陷改变算符的维数；并将该界应用于二维Ising/Potts模型做精确检验，以及三维O(N)模型中取向不同的pinning field缺陷直和（用fuzzy-sphere计算最低畴壁维数），另外应用于AdS/BCFT。

Result: Ising与Potts模型给出精确验证；在三维O(N)模型中，单一缺陷数据排除了O(2)、O(3)中三种及以上取向、O(4)中四种及以上取向的稳定杂质序（与取向几何无关），并要求O(3)中相对角至120°、O(4)中至109.47°的每对稳定pinning field缺陷之间存在相关的畴壁算符；N=2,3,4的fuzzy-sphere直接计算与界一致，表明该类杂质中仅两态序在O(3)、O(4)中仍可能稳定，O(2)中则无。AdS/BCFT中固定不等膜张力时，轻的边界条件改变算符要求边界上存在额外的轻原初算符，且其边界熵更大。

Conclusion: 缺陷熵与缺陷改变算符维数构成的解析上界可作为判定缺陷上相关算符存在性与稳定性的有效工具，并在一系列模型中给出非平凡的稳定性排除结论。

Abstract: Conformal defects describe impurities and boundaries at criticality. Their stability depends on the scaling dimensions of operators localized on the defects, but these dimensions are often difficult to determine. We derive analytic upper bounds on the lowest nonvacuum scaling dimension using only two defect entropies and the dimension of a defect-changing operator. The bounds identify when relevant operators must appear on the defect with larger defect entropy. They follow from the conformal bootstrap of four-point functions of defect-changing operators and apply to unitary conformal line defects and to conformal boundaries in two-dimensional CFTs. The Ising and Potts models provide exact checks. In three-dimensional $O(N)$ models, we apply the bounds to direct sums of pinning field defects with symmetry-related orientations. Data from a single such defect exclude stable impurity order with three or more orientations in $O(2)$ and $O(3)$, or four or more in $O(4)$, for any geometry of the orientations, and require a relevant domain-wall operator between individually stable pinning field defects at every nonzero relative angle up to $120^\circ$ in $O(3)$ and up to $109.47^\circ$ in $O(4)$. A direct fuzzy-sphere computation of the lowest domain-wall dimension at different relative angles for $N=2,3,4$ is consistent with these bounds and indicates that, within this class of impurities, only two-state order remains a candidate for stable order in $O(3)$ and $O(4)$, while none survives in $O(2)$. A separate application to AdS/BCFT shows that, at fixed unequal brane tensions, a light boundary-condition-changing operator requires additional light primaries on the boundary with larger boundary entropy.

</details>


### [106] [Density kernel and temporal quantum information probes in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08592)
*Johanna Erdmenger,Maximilian Gaschler,Jonathan Karl,Arnab Kundu,Georg Setzer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在双标度SYK模型中用精确的弦希尔伯特空间构造时间维纠缠密度核，研究跨时间量子关联的诊断量，并给出其与半经典引力及Krylov空间波前图像的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠定义局限于固定时刻的希尔伯特空间二分，近期工作借由时间关联函数构造的非厄米密度核将其推广到多时刻，以刻画因果响应、时间非经典性与量子动力学。作者希望在DSSYK这一可精确求解的模型中具体构造该核并系统研究其诊断量，从而在量子与半经典引力区间内可控地考察时间量子关联，并为密度核寻找几何解释。

Method: 利用DSSYK的精确弦希尔伯特空间表述构造时间密度核；借助弦空间的直和结构引入粗粒化，去除不同弦扇区之间的相干性，使核所对应的度量刻画信息在弦空间子区域间的动力学转移；并通过与DSSYK的Lanczos系数对应的、位置依赖的跃迁参数，建立Krylov空间中的波前图像来解释传播时间。

Result: 发现减小λ会同时增强时间关联在弦扇区间的传播以及固定扇区内记忆的丢失，且这种加速的时间动力学与小λ极限下半经典引力描述的出现相吻合；依弦空间划分方式不同，探测量分别呈现饱和、先增长后衰减或暂时性记忆丢失等行为；特征传播时间可由具位置依赖跃迁参数的Krylov空间波前图像很好地刻画。

Conclusion: 结合DSSYK的量子正弦-膨胀子引力描述及其与de Sitter全息（dS）的联系，该工作为跨量子与半经典引力区间探测时间量子关联提供了可控框架，并为进一步赋予密度核几何解释提供了动机。

Abstract: Entanglement is conventionally defined through a Hilbert space bipartition on a fixed time slice. Recent work has generalized this notion to multiple times through a non-Hermitian density kernel constructed from temporal correlation functions, encoding causal response, temporal non-classicality and quantum dynamics. We construct this kernel in the double-scaled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (DSSYK) model using its exact chord Hilbert space formulation and study the resulting temporal information diagnostics. The direct sum structure of chord space naturally introduces a coarse-graining under which coherence between different chord sectors is removed. We show that the corresponding kernel measures quantify how information is dynamically transferred across subregions in the chord Hilbert space. We find that decreasing $\lambda$ enhances the propagation of temporal correlation across chord sectors, as well as the loss of memory within a fixed sector. This accelerated temporal dynamics in the small $\lambda$ limit coincides with the emergence of a semiclassical gravity description. Depending on the chord space partition, the probes exhibit either saturation, a growth followed by a decay, or temporary memory loss. The characteristic propagation time is nicely captured by a wavefront picture in Krylov space, with position-dependent hopping parameters that are determined by the DSSYK Lanczos coefficients. Finally, given the quantum sine-dilaton gravity description of DSSYK and its connections to de Sitter holography, our results provide a controlled setting for probing temporal quantum correlations across quantum and semiclassical gravitational regimes, and motivate a geometric interpretation of the density kernel.

</details>


### [107] [NLO corrections to charged-current correlators near extremality: non-Abelian hydrodynamics meets AdS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08628)
*Matti Järvinen,Elias Kiritsis,Francesco Nitti,Andrea Olzi,Edwan Préau*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在近极端全息等离子体中，解析计算了含夸克化学势与同位旋化学势的纵向带电流关联函数，采用超越标准流体力学的次领头阶展开，并给出与数值结果高度吻合的准正规模谱与谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞背景下 AdS$_2$ 喉部会产生一整套红外模式，其与流体力学极点之间的相互作用（碰撞与抬升）难以用标准流体力学描述，需要在 $\omega,k,T,\mu_3\ll\mu_q$ 的层级下发展超越领头阶的解析方法，以扩展谱函数适用区间。

Method: 在带电于夸克数与同位旋的双荷 AdS$_5$ Reissner-Nordström 黑洞（低温、近极端）背景上研究规范场涨落，于 $\omega,k,T,\mu_3\ll\mu_q$ 的流体力学展开中计算纵向带电流关联函数的次领头阶解析表达式。

Result: 得到的准正规模公式在零同位旋化学势下重现扩散极点与红外谱的碰撞，有限 $\mu_3$ 使该碰撞被抬升（避免交叉）；解析谱与完整数值结果精确一致，次领头阶关联函数把谱函数与数值的符合范围推广到更高频率与动量。

Conclusion: 次领头阶流体力学展开能统一描述流体力学极点与 AdS$_2$ 红外模式的相互作用，显著优于标准流体力学区间 $\omega,k,\mu_3\ll T$ 的适用范围，为强耦合近极端等离子体的输运与谱性质提供可靠解析工具。

Abstract: We analytically compute the longitudinal charged-current correlator in a near-extremal holographic plasma with quark and isospin chemical potentials, $\mu_q$ and $\mu_3$ respectively. To this end, we study fluctuations of gauge fields around a low-temperature AdS$_5$ Reissner-Nordström black hole charged both under the quark number and the isospin. The result is obtained at next-to-leading order in a hydrodynamic expansion where apart from the temperature and isospin chemical potential, the frequency and momenta are small compared to the quark chemical potential, $\omega,k,T,\mu_3\ll\mu_q$. This approach captures the interplay between the hydrodynamic-like pole and the tower of infrared modes produced by the AdS$_2$ throat controlling the geometry of the black hole near extremality. At zero isospin chemical potential, the resulting quasinormal-mode formula reproduces the collision of the diffusive pole with the infrared spectrum, while the finite isospin chemical potential lifts this collision. The analytic spectrum agrees precisely with the full numerical results. The next-to-leading-order correlator also substantially improves the spectral function beyond the standard hydrodynamic regime $\omega,k,\mu_3\ll T$, extending the agreement with numerics to larger frequencies and momenta.

</details>


### [108] [Edge dynamics of covariant higher-rank Chern-Simons theory of fractons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09786)
*Erica Bertolini,Matteo Carrega,Nicola Maggiore,Daniel Sacco Shaikh,Maria Florencia Semino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究2+1维分形子协变高阶Chern-Simons理论在平面边界存在时的边界结构，导出两类边界流代数、局域解与诱导作用，并阐明边界条件对模速度与正能分支的选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶（分形子）Chern-Simons理论的体拓扑性质已有研究，但其边界动力学、边界守恒律与Kac-Moody代数的层级结构、以及体-边界混合等时对易子的因果性尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 利用标量规范对称性对应的微分生成元，取泛函场方程的单标量Ward组合及其第零、第一法向矩，得到两个积分边界恒等式；通过泛函微分计算混合体-边界等时对易子；并在解局域约束 w_i=∂_iχ、h_ij=∂_i∂_jξ+η_ijφ 后，将解耦情形下的诱导作用写为两个扇区的直和并作手征约化分析。

Result: 第零矩给出无迹二秩流，满足分形子双散度守恒律及高阶Kac-Moody代数；第一矩显式给出法向-切向场 w_i=h_2i，并给出普通守恒矢量流与标准U(1) Kac-Moody代数。w_1与h_11的混合等时对易子在有限法向距离非零、在内禀边界极限为零；边界流与规范不变场强空间分量的等时对易子为零，微观因果性满足，两扇区解耦时延迟响应恒为零。

Conclusion: 边界诱导作用在解耦情形下是 w_i 扇区的手征玻色子与 h_ij 扇区（φ为辅助场、ψ=∂_1ξ为传播场）的直和，后者在正能分支上具非手征Luttinger描述并可手征约化；边界条件决定 w_i 模的速度与正能分支，h_ij 扇区的相容性分析选取正能分支，正能分支上两个手征模同向传播且传播方向与κ符号相反。

Abstract: We study the covariant higher-rank Chern-Simons theory of fractons in 2+1 dimensions in the presence of a planar boundary. The differential generator associated with the scalar gauge symmetry singles out a single scalar Ward combination of the functional field equations. Its zeroth and first normal moments give two integrated boundary identities. The zeroth moment yields a traceless rank-two current obeying a fractonic double-divergence conservation law and a higher-rank Kac-Moody algebra. The first moment makes the normal-tangential field $w_i=h_{2i}$ explicit and yields an ordinarily conserved vector current with a standard $U(1)$ Kac-Moody algebra. Functional differentiation also produces a mixed equal-time bulk-boundary commutator between $w_1$ and $h_{11}$: it is non-vanishing at finite normal distance and vanishes in the intrinsic boundary limit. The equal-time commutators of the boundary current with the spatial components of the gauge-invariant field strength vanish, so microcausality is satisfied; when the boundary parameters are such that the two boundary sectors decouple, its retarded response vanishes at all times. Locally, the boundary constraints are solved by $w_i=\partial_i\chi$ and $h_{ij}=\partial_i\partial_j\xi+\eta_{ij}\phi$. When the boundary parameters are such that the two sectors decouple, the induced action is the direct sum of a chiral boson in the $w_i$ sector and a higher-rank sector in which $\phi$ is auxiliary and $\psi=\partial_1\xi$ is the propagating field. The latter has a non-chiral Luttinger description on its positive-energy branch and admits a chiral reduction. The boundary conditions determine the velocity and positivity branch of the $w_i$ mode, while the compatibility analysis in the $h_{ij}$ sector selects the positive-energy branch. On the positive-energy branch the two chiral modes co-propagate with orientation opposite to the sign of $\kappa$.

</details>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Synchronization transitions in Kuramoto oscillators with mixed interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09564)
*Abhishek Sharma,Ayushi Suman,Mehernaz Anvari,Manish Dev Shrimali,Charo I. del Genio,Sarika Jalan*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文在具有混合阶（pairwise与higher-order）相互作用的复杂网络上研究Kuramoto振子的有限尺寸效应，用数值模拟揭示系统规模、平均度与相互作用强度如何改变同步转变行为，并与全局耦合无限系统的理论结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于Kuramoto模型从非相干到相干转变的研究几乎都假设振子之间为全局耦合，而许多真实系统具有特定的网络结构。作者旨在填补这一空白：考察网络结构下、尤其是含混合阶相互作用时的有限尺寸效应，检验已知全局耦合无限系统理论结果的适用性。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，构建具有混合阶相互作用的网络化Kuramoto振子系综（有限规模），系统改变系统尺寸（振子数）、节点平均度以及相互作用强度等参数，观测同步转变的特征（如转变点位置、临界性质），并将结果与全局耦合无限系统下的理论预测进行对照。

Result: 模拟结果表明，同步转变行为显著依赖于系统尺寸、平均度和相互作用强度；这些有限尺度与网络结构因素使转变偏离全局耦合无限系统理论所预言的普适行为。

Conclusion: 全局耦合的无限系统理论不足以刻画真实网络中的同步转变；混合阶相互作用网络的有限尺寸效应需被显式考虑。本文为网络化Kuramoto模型的后续理论工作提供了数值基准。

Abstract: The Kuramoto model is a paradigmatic system for emergent synchronization phenomena, and significant efforts have been made to investigate its transition from incoherent to coherent dynamics. However, previous studies have almost exclusively assumed global coupling between the oscillators, even though many real-world systems are organized in specific network structures. Here, we fill this gap by examining finite-size effects in Kuramoto oscillator ensembles on networks with mixed-order interactions. Using numerical simulations, we show how parameters such as the size of the system, the average degree of the nodes and the strength of the interactions alter the behaviour of the transition to synchronization with respect to the known theoretical results that are derived for globally coupled infinite systems.

</details>

### [110] [Spontaneous Formation of Lateral Defect-Density Gradient in CVD-Grown MoS2 Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09103)
*M. Kostka,H.-S. Chen,C.-Y. Hsiao,Y.-R. Chu,J. Mach,M. Bartošík,J. Šťastný,T. Šikola,W.-C. Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文报道CVD生长MoS2单晶片内自发形成的缺陷密度梯度，通过PL/Raman面扫与激子峰解卷积确认硫空位引入的电子掺杂是光响应空间变化的主因，机理归因于成核中心附近前驱体耗尽，为在同一单样品内连续调变缺陷密度提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料缺陷工程可调控电学、光学与催化性质，但缺陷态MoS2因可控缺陷制备复杂且可重复性差而研究不足，亟需便捷、连续可调的缺陷密度研究平台。

Method: 化学气相沉积（CVD）生长MoS2薄片；利用光致发光（PL）与拉曼光谱mapping表征单片的横向缺陷梯度分布；对激子发光峰进行解卷积分析；结合拉曼模位移推断缺陷类型与载流子掺杂。

Result: PL与Raman mapping显示缺陷密度在单片内呈横向梯度分布；激子峰解卷积表明电子掺杂主导光学响应的空间变化；该掺杂源于CVD生长中形成的硫空位，并由拉曼模位移佐证；机制解释为成核中心附近前驱体耗尽导致缺陷密度渐变。

Conclusion: CVD生长可自发产生横向缺陷密度梯度，构成在单一样品内系统表征连续缺陷密度区间的便捷平台；所识别的硫空位驱动表面活化对后续催化应用具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Defect engineering in two-dimensional materials is a powerful strategy for tuning electronic, optical, and catalytic properties. While pristine molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) has been thoroughly studied, defective MoS2 remains poorly understood due to the complexity and inconsistency of controlled defect creation. Here, we report a defect-density gradient that forms spontaneously in MoS2 flakes during chemical vapor deposition (CVD) growth. Photoluminescence and Raman spectroscopy mapping reveal the lateral arrangement of this gradient across a single flake. Deconvolution of the excitonic photoluminescence emission identifies electron doping as the key factor governing the spatial variation in optical response. This doping likely originates from sulfur vacancies formed during CVD growth, as further supported by the observed Raman mode shifts. We propose that this gradient arises from precursor depletion near the nucleation center during growth, resulting in a gradual variation in defect density. This lateral defect structure provides a convenient platform for systematically characterizing defective MoS2 across a continuous range of defect densities within a single sample. The sulfur-vacancy-driven surface activation identified here can also be important for future catalytic applications.

</details>


### [111] [A Monte Carlo study of thermal conductivity in black phosphorene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09149)
*L. O. Vilyaev,D. V. Zavyalov,V. I. Konchenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用蒙特卡洛方法结合Green-Kubo关系，通过统计建模电子在声学与光学声子上的运动与散射过程，估算了黑磷烯的电子热导率，所获结果在理论结果与实验数据之间建立了更严谨的支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑磷烯（black phosphorene）具有显著的各向异性结构，其热输运性质的方向依赖性对器件热管理至关重要；此前关于其各向异性热导率的理论工作尚缺乏严格论证，需要更可靠的计算框架加以验证与支撑。

Method: 将蒙特卡洛方法与Green-Kubo关系相结合，对电子在声学和光学声子上的运动与散射进行统计建模；其中散射概率以解析形式表达，进而由速度自相关或热流关联计算电子热导率。

Result: 热导率呈现明显的各向异性：沿扶手椅（armchair）方向的热导率数值比锯齿（zigzag）方向高约一个数量级，与相关材料的实验数据一致；同时该建模为以往关于黑磷烯各向异性热导率的理论结果提供了更严格的论证。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛–Green-Kubo联合框架能够有效描述黑磷烯中电子–声子散射主导的热输运，并可靠地再现其强各向异性特征，为后续理论验证与器件设计提供依据。

Abstract: In this work the electronic thermal conductivity of black phosphorene is studied. Using the Monte Carlo method together with the Green-Kubo relations, a statistical modelling of the motion and scattering of electrons on acoustic and optical phonons is performed to estimate the electronic thermal conductivity. The scattering probability is expressed analytically. The obtained results show a pronounced anisotropy of the thermal conductivity: the thermal conductivity values along the armchair direction are an order of magnitude larger than those along the zigzag direction, in agreement with the experimental data for related materials. In addition, the modelling allowed a more rigorous justification of some results of previous theoretical works on the anisotropic thermal conductivity of black phosphorene.

</details>


### [112] [Pressure-induced symmetry breaking and polarity in the van der Waals magnet CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09202)
*Amit Pawbake,Sitaram Ramakrishnan,Kseniia Mosina,Aljoscha Soll,Zdenek Sofer,Pierre Rodiere,Rolf Heid,Gaston Garbarino,Pierre Bouvier,Clement Faugeras*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合低温单晶X射线衍射、拉曼散射、光致发光及第一性原理计算，系统研究了CrSBr在高达12 GPa静水压下的结构与电子态演化，揭示了其级联结构相变与带隙类型转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrSBr是研究结构、磁性与电子性质耦合的重要范德华磁体平台，但其晶格对称性与电子态在压力和温度下的演化规律尚未解决，因此需要系统澄清。

Method: 在高达12 GPa的静水压条件下开展低温单晶X射线衍射、拉曼散射与光致发光测量，并结合第一性原理计算进行对照分析。

Result: 观察到级联压力诱导结构相变：约1.5 GPa时由正交Pmmn相转变为单斜P2₁/m相，随后约6.5 GPa时出现极性正交Pmm2相；对称性变化伴随声子异常以及直接带隙向间接带隙的交叉转变。

Conclusion: 压力可作为调控CrSBr结构、对称性与电子性质并实现耦合调节的有效手段。

Abstract: The van der Waals magnet CrSBr has emerged as a fascinating platform for exploring coupled structural, magnetic, and electronic phenomena. Yet, the evolution of its crystal symmetry and electronic states under pressure and temperature remains unresolved. To elucidate this we employ low-temperature single-crystal X-ray diffraction, Raman scattering and photoluminescence measurements under hydrostatic pressures of up to 12 GPa, complemented by first-principles  this http URL  observe a cascade of pressure-induced structural transitions from the orthorhombic $Pmmn$ to monoclinic $P2_1/m$ phase at $\approx$ 1.5 GPa, followed by the emergence of a polar orthorhombic $Pmm$2 phase $\approx$ 6.5 GPa. The change in symmetry is accompanied by phonon anomalies and a direct-to-indirect bandgap crossover. Our results demonstrate pressure as a knob to engineer structure, symmetry and electronic properties in CrSBr in a coupled manner.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Deep Learning Theory through the Lens of Statistical Physics: A Topical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09138)
*Wei Huang,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇从统计物理视角综述深度学习理论的topical review，用相变、临界性、自由能、平均场与动力平均场、核/高斯过程极限、随机矩阵与Fokker-Planck方法，系统整合宽网络平均场分析、损失景观的谱与几何视角、SGD训练的动力学描述，以及过参数化、泛化与表征变化的相变类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与深度学习的经验成功远超对其"如何学习与泛化"的理论理解；高维、非线性、强耦合的神经网络恰好是统计物理擅长描述的对象，而现有理论叙事（平均场、核极限、动力学、相变）彼此割裂、适用条件模糊，亟需统一框架厘清各近似的解释力与有效性边界。

Method: 采用统计物理式思维与方法的综述性综合：(1) 概念层——相与临界性、自由能视角；(2) 方法层——核极限与高斯过程极限、平均场与动力平均场理论、随机矩阵工具、Fokker-Planck形式；(3) 方法论心态——普适性、标度与带明确有效域的可解极限；并据此梳理四条研究线索并作对照整合。

Result: 梳理并对比了若干进展：宽网络的平均场分析、损失景观的谱与几何刻画、将随机梯度训练视为动力过程、以及用相变类比组织过参数化、泛化与表征变化等现象；并对不同（看似竞争的）叙事之间的关系与分歧给出定位。

Conclusion: 这些视角共同构成一个统一物理框架，把表征学习视为高维系统中的集体现象，并衔接梯度优化的确定性（平均场/常微分方程）与随机（涨落/Fokker-Planck）两个区域；框架的核心价值在于明确每个近似"解释什么、何时成立"。

Abstract: The recent success of foundation models and deep learning has far outpaced our theoretical understanding of how large-scale neural networks learn and generalize. Statistical physics provides a natural language for describing such high-dimensional, nonlinear, and strongly coupled systems, offering concepts such as mean-field theory, phase transitions, energy landscapes, and stochastic dynamics. In this topical review, we adopt a statistical-physics way of thinking -- from concepts including phases and criticality, and free-energy viewpoints, to methods such as kernel and Gaussian-process limits, mean-field and dynamical mean-field theories, random-matrix tools, and Fokker-Planck formalisms, and to a mindset emphasizing universality, scaling, and tractable limits with explicit validity regimes. We survey recent progress on understanding modern deep learning through this lens, including mean-field analyses of wide networks, spectral and geometric perspectives on loss landscapes, stochastic-gradient-based training viewed as a dynamical process, and phase-transition analogies that organize phenomena such as overparameterization, generalization, and representation change. Our goal is to synthesize these threads into a coherent framework that clarifies what each approximation explains, when it is expected to hold, and how apparently competing narratives relate or diverge. Together, these perspectives form a unified physical framework that views representation learning as a collective phenomenon in high-dimensional systems, bridging deterministic and stochastic regimes of gradient-based optimization.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Features of Non-Local Quantum Correlations in Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08776)
*James W. T. Keeble,Caroline E. P. Robin,Federico Rocco,Martin J. Savage*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该工作在260余个原子核的壳模型基态中，以质子-中子二分为准则计算稳定子Rényi熵、非局域magic、纠缠谱反平坦度与纠缠熵，验证了4F_A ≤ M^NL_lin的界并发现奇奇核具有独特的纠缠与magic模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子多体系统同时受纠缠与非稳定化性（magic）制约；非局域magic刻画无法通过局域幺正变换消除的magic部分，是超越纠缠的量子复杂性度量。作者希望检验其与纠缠谱反平坦度的下界关系，并刻画核力所生成的复杂性。

Method: 采用质子/中子扇区二分的壳模型基态，对超过260个偶偶、奇偶与奇奇核系统计算稳定子Rényi熵、非局域magic M^NL_α（含M^NL_lin）、纠缠谱反平坦度F_A及纠缠熵，并检验下界关系及其饱和行为。

Result: 对所研究的每个核，下界4F_A ≤ M^NL_lin均成立，且在可分态极限下趋于饱和；奇奇核的纠缠与magic分布模式明显区别于偶偶和奇偶核，显示出质子-中子量子关联的特征印记。

Conclusion: 纠缠谱反平坦度可作为非局域magic的可靠下界指示量；核壳模型基态中magic与纠缠的模式差异（尤其奇奇核）为核相互作用生成的量子复杂性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Classical simulation of quantum many-body systems is challenged by the combined effects of entanglement and non-stabilizerness (magic). Non-local magic, $M^{\mathrm{NL}}_\alpha$, quantifies the component of non-stabilizerness that cannot be removed by local unitary transformations and provides a measure of quantum complexity beyond entanglement alone. It is bounded from below by the antiflatness of the entanglement spectrum, ${F}_A$. We calculate stabilizer Rényi entropies, non-local magic, entanglement-spectrum antiflatness, and entanglement entropy in shell-model ground states of more than 260 even-even, odd-even, and odd-odd nuclei, using a bipartition into proton and neutron sectors. We find that the previously established bound $4{F}_A\leq{M}^{\mathrm{NL}}_{\mathrm{lin}}$ holds for every nucleus studied and approaches saturation in the limit of separable states. Odd-odd nuclei exhibit markedly different patterns of entanglement and magic compared with even-even and odd-even systems, revealing distinctive signatures of proton-neutron quantum correlations and providing new insight into the complexity generated by nuclear interactions.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Suppression of gel formation by spontaneous fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09537)
*Sergey Matveev*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 研究带乘法核并附加每个粒子以恒定速率λ自发碎裂为单体的离散Smoluchowski方程，证明λ≥4时碎裂抑制凝胶化，给出二阶矩与单体浓度n₁(t)的解析表达式及稳态粒径分布的递推算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法核下的Smoluchowski凝聚方程会导致凝胶化（质量向无穷大粒子输运），而实际颗粒系统中常伴随碎裂过程。作者希望定量刻画恒定速率自发碎裂项对凝胶化的抑制作用及其对稳态粒径分布的影响。

Method: 在离散Smoluchowski方程中引入乘法核与常数速率λ的自发碎裂（碎裂为单体）项；通过解析推导得到二阶矩和单体浓度n₁(t)的闭式表达；由此构造递推关系计算λ≥4时的稳态粒径分布；并用基于FFT计算右端项、自适应步长二阶Runge–Kutta方法的数值求解器进行验证。

Result: 证明λ≥4时碎裂项抑制凝胶化；得到二阶矩和单体浓度n₁(t)的解析表达式；该表达式导出可达到机器精度的稳态粒径分布递推算法；数值模拟与解析/半解析结果一致，验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 恒定速率自发碎裂在λ≥4时足以消除乘法核凝聚中的凝胶化现象，体系趋于具有明确稳态分布的平衡态；解析、半解析与数值结果相互印证，为含碎裂的凝聚动力学提供了精确可算的框架。

Abstract: In this work we study the discrete Smoluchowski equation with a multiplicative kernel and an additional term describing the spontaneous shattering of each particle into monomers at a constant rate $\lambda$. We show that the introduction of fragmentation suppresses gelation for $\lambda \ge 4$. We derive analytical expressions for the second moment and the monomer concentration $n_1(t)$. These expressions lead to a recurrence relation allowing the steady-state particle size distribution for $\lambda \ge 4$ to be calculated with machine precision. We validate our analytical and semi-analytical results via numerical simulations using a solver that employs the Fast Fourier Transform for computation of the right-hand side and a second-order Runge-Kutta method with adaptive time steps.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Spin to Orbital Angular Momentum Conversion in the Neutron Nucleus Weak Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09677)
*Niels Geerits,Stephan Sponar,Hartmut Lemmel,Andrea Piovano,Kyle Steffen,William Michael Snow*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 慢中子穿过¹³⁹La时，n-p弱相互作用会产生极小的宇称非守恒自旋旋转（可达2.2×10⁻³ rad·cm⁻¹），该效应在0.7 eV的p波共振处被大幅放大。本文实验证明该旋转通过中子轨道角动量的产生来满足总角动量守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇称非守恒中子自旋旋转必须遵守总角动量守恒，但角动量如何在自旋与轨道自由度之间重新分配此前缺乏直接实验检验。作者希望厘清该守恒机制究竟是自旋角动量转移给核还是转化为中子的轨道角动量。

Method: 利用¹³⁹La在0.7 eV处的p波共振（宇称奇异性显著放大的共振），通过核散射实现宇称非守恒自旋旋转；制备初始处于纵向自旋-轨道态（螺旋度本征态）的中子束，测量其旋转前后总角动量的变化。

Result: 观察到宇称非守恒自旋旋转伴随中子轨道角动量的产生，且初始纵向自旋-轨道态的中子总角动量在旋转后保持不变，即角动量在自旋与轨道自由度间重新分配而非消失。

Conclusion: 宇称非守恒中子自旋旋转通过产生轨道角动量而非改变总角动量来实现，验证了弱相互作用下的角动量守恒机制，并表明中子自旋-轨道操控可用于探测宇称非守恒效应。

Abstract: The neutron nucleus weak interaction generates a small parity non conserving rotation of the neutron spin as the neutron moves through matter. This parity-odd neutron rotary power can reach up to 2.2e-3 rad * cm-1 for slow neutrons in 139La, which possesses a p-wave resonance at 0.7 eV where the parity-odd asymmetry is greatly amplified. This spin rotation must conserve total angular momentum. Here we show that this conservation occurs through neutron orbital angular momentum generation. We experimentally demonstrate that a neutron prepared initially in a longitudinal spin-orbit state experiences no change in total angular momentum.

</details>
