### [117] [Slow structures, fast degrees of freedom, and the morphology of chess openings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09710)
*Marc Barthelemy,Manlio De Domenico*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文将国际象棋开局分解为"慢变量"（兵结构）与"快变量"（子力重组），基于1749个达到10个半回合深度的命名变例构建开局空间的定量形态学，并以兵结构为节点、单步兵转化为边构建网络，发现139/149个结构属于同一连通分量（涵盖98%局面），且具显著模块性，划分为8个与经典兵形对应的社区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开局分类依赖命名体系，把开局视为彼此孤立的类别，难以定量比较不同名开局所产生的局面。作者希望利用兵的不逆移动与子力快速重组这一"慢—快自由度分离"特性，为开局空间建立可量化的描述与结构刻画。

Method: 从3810个命名变例中取1749个到达N=10半回合的变例，归为619个不同局面与435个联合兵结构；用衡量兵推进承诺、空间组织、封锁与张力、子力活跃度与中心化的观测量刻画局面；以白方兵结构为节点、单步兵移动或吃子为连边构建网络，并做连通性与模块度（社区检测）分析。

Result: 各观测量部分独立，表明开局空间是多维的；149个观测到的白兵结构中有139个位于同一连通分量，覆盖约98%的局面；网络呈统计学显著的模块组织，识别出8个社区，其代表结构与常见兵形（如法兰西、卡罗-卡恩等经典形态）相符。

Conclusion: 经典兵形并非彼此孤立的分类，而是一个连通结构景观中的不同区域。该方法补充了传统开局分类学：既可揭示不同命名开局之间的相似性，也为比较其产生的局面提供了定量基础。

Abstract: Chess openings naturally separate into slow and fast degrees of freedom: pawns evolve through irreversible moves, whereas pieces can be rapidly reorganized within the resulting structure. We exploit this separation to construct a quantitative morphology of opening space. From a catalogue of 3810 named variations, 1749 reach a common depth of $N=10$ plies, corresponding to 619 distinct positions and only 435 joint pawn structures. We characterize these positions through observables measuring pawn commitment and spatial organization, blockade and tension, and piece activity and centralization. Their partial independence reveals a multidimensional opening space and in order to explore its structural organization, we construct a network whose nodes are distinct White pawn structures, connected when a single pawn move or capture transforms one into another. Remarkably, 139 of the 149 observed structures belong to a single connected component containing $98\%$ of all positions. This network exhibits a statistically significant modular organization, with eight communities whose representative structures correspond to familiar pawn formations. Rather than forming isolated categories, these formations emerge as distinct regions of a connected structural landscape. Our approach thus complements the traditional opening taxonomy by revealing similarities between differently named openings and providing a quantitative basis for comparing the positions they produce.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [A proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09773)
*David Turturean*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 作者证明了 Aaronson–Ambainis 猜想：离散立方体上低次数有界多项式必有影响度不低于 $c\,\mathrm{Var}(f)^{5/2}/d^{6}$ 的变量，从而说明查询模型中的超多项式量子加速必须依赖输入结构（如 promise）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson 与 Ambainis 曾猜想：离散立方体上每个低次数有界多项式都存在一个"有影响变量"（influential variable）。该猜想的意义在于，它可推出任何 $T$ 次查询量子算法的接受概率，都能在多数输入上被 $\mathrm{poly}(T)$ 次查询的经典算法近似——即查询模型下若不存在输入结构假设，量子算法无法获得超多项式加速。本文旨在直接证明该猜想。

Method: 证明思路为初等（elementary），融合并借鉴他人已有工作的技术步骤，对定义在 $\{-1,1\}^n$ 上、次数不超过 $d\ge1$、取值于 $[-1,1]$ 的任意函数 $f$，建立影响度下界 $\max_i \mathrm{Inf}_i(f)\ge c\,\mathrm{Var}(f)^{5/2}/d^{6}$（$c>0$ 为绝对常数）。在原始表述下（$\{0,1\}^n$ 上取值于 $[0,1]$、方差至少 $\varepsilon$ 的 $d$ 次多项式），该结论给出影响度至少 $(\varepsilon/d)^{7}$。

Result: 猜想被完全证明。所得显式界：一般形式为 $\mathrm{Var}(f)^{5/2}/d^{6}$ 量级（乘以绝对常数），原表述形式下为 $(\varepsilon/d)^{7}$。

Conclusion: 正如 Aaronson 与 Ambainis 所预期的，查询模型中的超多项式量子加速必须依赖输入结构（如 promise）；在没有结构假设的一般情形下，量子优势在经典多项式查询可模拟范围内受限。

Abstract: Aaronson and Ambainis conjectured that every bounded polynomial of low degree on the discrete cube has an influential variable. They showed that the conjecture implies that the acceptance probability of every quantum algorithm making $T$ queries can be approximated on most inputs by a classical algorithm making $\mathrm{poly}(T)$ queries. We prove their conjecture: every function $f\colon\{-1,1\}^n\to[-1,1]$ of degree at most $d\ge1$ has a variable of influence at least $c\,\operatorname{Var}(f)^{5/2}/d^{6}$, where $c>0$ is an absolute constant. In the original formulation, for polynomials of degree at most $d$ with values in $[0,1]$ on $\{0,1\}^n$ and variance at least $\varepsilon$, this gives a variable of influence at least $(\varepsilon/d)^{7}$. The proof is elementary, with steps inspired by works of others. Thus, as Aaronson and Ambainis anticipated, superpolynomial quantum speedups in the query model require structure, such as a promise on the inputs.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Structure is in the zoom: probing neural symmetry through dimensionality scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08506)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 神经维度标度指数并非编码的固有属性，而是"群体+探针"的联合量；可精确分解为随机子采样的"地板"与刺激类别轴带来的"位移"，二者之和决定了指数的真实含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究把神经维度标度指数当作表征编码的内在属性，用于比较表征、将模型与皮层对标；但同一份记录（小鼠V1，11,311个神经元）仅因降采样方式不同就给出0.25、0.30、0.35等不同结果，说明该指数的可解释性存疑，亟需厘清其来源与构成。

Method: 提出指数的精确分解：floor（同规模随机子集给出的基线）+ shift（由声明的刺激类别轴附加的位移）。在光栅记录中分析刺激方向如何同时激活编码的两个对称扇区（方向，半圈周期重复；朝向，不重复）；区分试次间波动与类别均值的作用；用两个可测量项（类别波动重叠度、均值相对波动的相对位置）解释shift；并在Allen Neuropixels、猕猴V1、XY晶格（温度翻转符号）与GPT-2（间接宾语电路head排序）上验证。发布开源工具rung。

Result: 常规随机子采样指数几乎全部由floor构成；刺激方向在所有光栅记录中都提升指数，其lift存在于方向间不同的试次间波动中，而线性解码器读取的类别均值反而拉低指数。两个测量项可预测shift的大小与符号：在Allen Neuropixels中类别均值多抬高指数，在猕猴V1亦然；XY晶格中温度按两预测项翻转符号；GPT-2中shift可对间接宾语电路的name-mover与backup head进行排序。

Conclusion: 报告中同时给出路径（path）、floor与shift的标度指数，才能说明探针发现了多少结构以及结构由什么承载；开源工具rung可对任意带标签的神经记录或语言模型层输出这三项指标。

Abstract: Scaling exponents of neural dimensionality are read as properties of the code, and used to compare representations and benchmark models against cortex. Yet one recording of 11,311 mouse V1 neurons gives 0.25, 0.30 or 0.35, depending only on how it is subsampled. We show that such an exponent measures the population and the probe together. We split it exactly into a floor, which random subsets of the same sizes give, and a shift, which a declared axis of stimulus classes adds. The usual random-subsample exponent is almost all floor. Stimulus direction lifts the exponent in every grating recording by reading both symmetry sectors of the code: orientation, which repeats every half turn, and direction, which does not. The lift lives in trial-to-trial fluctuations that differ between directions, while the class means, which linear decoders read, pull it down. Two measured terms, how much the classes' fluctuations overlap and how the means sit against them, account for the shift. They give its sign where it changes: in Allen Neuropixels recordings, whose class means mostly raise the exponent, and in macaque V1. In an XY lattice whose classes stay on one orbit, temperature flips the sign as the two terms say, and in GPT-2 the shift ranks the indirect-object circuit's name-mover and backup heads. Reported with its path, its floor and its shift, an exponent says how much structure a probe finds and what carries it. Our open tool, rung, reports all three for any labelled neural recording or language-model layer:  this https URL

</details>

### [120] [Entanglement in Collective Neutrino Oscillations on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08595)
*Katarina Bleau,Nikolina Ilic,Joachim Kopp,Ushak Rahaman,Xin Yue Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过在经典与量子硬件上显式模拟不同粒子数N的纠缠中微子系综多体动力学，并与基于Boltzmann型动理学方程的均值场方法对比，证明在大N极限下全多体模拟与均值场预测一致；同时给出均值场失效、量子纠缠具有唯象相关性的物理情形及其在天体环境中实现的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机被设想为研究超新星或中子星并合等稠密环境中中微子味振荡的下一代工具，但量子优势依赖中微子系综中存在量子纠缠；而此前有人认为在大中微子数密度极限下这种纠缠可忽略。本文旨在直接检验这一论断。

Method: 对粒子数N可变的纠缠中微子系综进行显式多体动力学模拟，同时在经典硬件与量子硬件上实现，并将结果与传统基于Boltzmann型动理学方程的"均值场"方法进行比较；此外发展了在经典与量子硬件上高效模拟纠缠中微子气体的新算法。

Result: 对简单玩具系统，解析与数值结果均表明：在大N极限下全多体模拟的预测与均值场方法一致，验证了均值场近似在大粒子数下的有效性。

Conclusion: 存在纠缠中微子系统无法仅由均值场动力学描述、因而量子纠缠具有唯象相关性的物理情形；文中讨论了这些情形在超新星核心或中子星并合中可能实现的条件。

Abstract: Simulations on a quantum computer have been propounded as a next-generation tool for studying neutrino flavour oscillations in dense environments like supernovae or neutron star mergers. However, quantum advantage hinges on the presence of quantum entanglement in the neutrino ensemble, and it has been argued that such entanglement becomes negligible in the limit of large neutrino number density. Here, we address this question by explicitly simulating the multiparticle dynamics of entangled neutrino ensembles with a varying number of particles, $N$, on both classical and quantum hardware, and comparing our results to those obtained with conventional ``mean-field'' methods based on Boltzmann-type kinetic equations. For simple toy systems, we demonstrate both analytically and numerically that, indeed, the predictions of full multiparticle simulations agree with those of mean-field methods in the large-$N$ limit. Along the way, we develop novel algorithms for the efficient simulation of entangled neutrino gases on both classical and quantum hardware. We then discuss physical situations in which an entangled neutrino system \emph{cannot} be described through mean-field dynamics alone and quantum entanglement therefore becomes phenomenologically relevant. We comment on the conditions under which these scenarios may be realized in supernova cores or neutron star mergers.

</details>


### [121] [Spiral momentum spectra and spin asymmetry in vacuum pair production driven by phase-modulated elliptically polarized electric fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08899)
*Li-Yi Yang,Hong-Hao Fan,Jing-He Huang,Shuo Yang,Bai-Song Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于量子动理学方程研究相位调制椭圆偏振电场中的真空正负电子对产生，揭示调制形式、幅度、频率与椭圆率对自旋分辨动量谱及自旋不对称性的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空正负电子对产生是强场量子电动力学的基本过程，但相位调制与椭圆偏振联合作用下自旋分辨动量谱的图案演化及自旋不对称性尚不清楚，需要系统考察调制参数对谱结构与自旋通道选择的影响。

Method: 采用量子动理学方程（quantum kinetic equations）求解相位调制椭圆偏振电场中的对产生过程，系统扫描调制幅度、调制频率、椭圆率与调制形式（余弦型、正弦型），分析自旋分辨的动量谱与自旋不对称度。

Result: 余弦型调制可将环形动量分布扭曲为螺旋图案，该变形对调制幅度和频率呈非单调依赖，在圆偏振下最显著；横向动量面内自旋分辨产额偏向与场手性方向一致的自旋通道，反转场手性会反转优势通道而总产额不变。正弦型调制基本保持环形与镜像对称谱，但其自旋不对称度对调制幅度的依赖强于余弦型。

Conclusion: 相位调制与椭圆率可作为调控真空对产生动量谱图案的有效手段，且相位调制还能改变自旋不对称程度，为自旋分辨的强场对产生调控提供途径。

Abstract: We study vacuum electron--positron pair production in phase-modulated elliptically polarized electric fields using the quantum kinetic equations. We analyze how the modulation amplitude, frequency, ellipticity, and modulation form affect the spin-resolved momentum spectra and the spin asymmetry. Cosine-type modulation can deform ring-shaped momentum distributions into spiral patterns. This deformation depends nonmonotonically on the modulation amplitude and frequency and is most pronounced for circular polarization. In the transverse momentum plane, the spin-resolved yields favor the spin channel along the direction of field handedness. Reversing the field handedness would reverse the favored channel while leaving the total yield unchanged. Sine-type modulation preserves ring-shaped and mirror-symmetric spectra approximately, however, it leads to a stronger dependence of the spin asymmetry on the modulation amplitude than cosine-type modulation does. These results show that phase modulation and ellipticity can control the momentum spectra patterns in vacuum pair production. Moreover, phase modulation can also affect the degree of spin asymmetry.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Fortuitous Cancellation of Competing Effects in the Free Energy of Water Autoionization: Long-Range Electrostatics, System Size, and Nuclear Quantum Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08874)
*Samuel Varner,Yinan Xu,Yezhi Jin,Gustavo R. Pérez-Lemus,Pablo F. Zubieta Rico,Zhiying Yi,Joan M. Montes de Oca,Paul F. Nealey,Seth B. Darling,Zhen-Gang Wang,Juan J. de Pablo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 结合机器学习原子间势（MLIP）与谱自适应偏置力增强采样，将质子转移与水线构型与自由能面关联，系统考察长程静电、体系大小、温度和核量子运动对水的电离热力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水的自电离（K_w≈10^-14）是酸碱平衡与生命化学的基础，但形成并分离离子的自由能代价的分子起源仍不清楚，主要受限于难以足够精细地模拟水的反应性。

Method: 将机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）与谱自适应偏置力（spectral adaptive biasing force）增强采样相结合；构建从中性水到超出溶剂分隔离子对（SSIP）的自由能面，并分析长程静电、体系尺寸、温度与核量子效应的竞争作用。

Result: 自由能面揭示过渡态中质子由三元水线的第二、第三分子共享；接触离子对不稳定，SSIP仅弱亚稳，故确定pK_w需更长的水线。长程静电使3 Å以外离子分离的自由能升高；力误差相当的模型在该区域给出显著不同的自由能曲线。增大体系尺寸降低pK_w，核量子效应使其更接近实验值，并重现D2O相对H2O更高的pK_w。

Conclusion: 为在有限晶胞中带电与极性基团反应、扩散的MLIP模拟的热力学结果提供基准；并说明各竞争效应的相互抵消可使模拟即使遗漏若干物理贡献，仍得到接近实验的电离常数。

Abstract: Few molecular processes are more fundamental to chemistry than the autoionization of water, whose equilibrium constant, $K_w \approx 10^{-14}$, underlies acid-base equilibria and the chemistry of life. Yet the molecular origins of the free energy cost of forming and separating ions remain incompletely understood. Efforts to explain it have been limited by the difficulty of modeling water reactivity in sufficient detail. Here we combine machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) with spectral adaptive biasing force enhanced sampling to connect proton transfer and water wire configurations to the underlying free energy landscape. We identify competing influences of long-range electrostatics, system size, temperature, and nuclear quantum motion on the predicted ionization thermodynamics. The free energy surface extends from neutral water to ions separated beyond the solvent-separated ion pair (SSIP). It reveals a transition state in which a proton is shared between the second and third molecules of a three-membered water wire. The contact ion pair is unstable and the SSIP only weakly metastable, so determining pK$_w$ requires sampling longer wires. Long-range electrostatics raise the free energy at ion separations beyond $3\,\mathrm{\mathring{A}}$. Models with comparable force errors give substantially different free energy profiles in this region. Increasing system size lowers the calculated pK$_w$, while nuclear quantum effects bring it closer to experiment and reproduce the higher pK$_w$ of D$_2$O relative to H$_2$O. These results establish a baseline for interpreting thermodynamic results from MLIP simulations in which charged and polar groups react and diffuse in finite cells. Our analysis also explains how cancellation among competing effects can yield ionization constants close to experiment even when simulations omit several physical contributions.

</details>


### [123] [Propagating the Hierarchical Equations of Motion using the Multi-Layer Multi-Configurational Time-Dependent Hartree method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09637)
*Robin L. Grether,Samuel L. Rudge,Martin Mäck,Riley J. Preston,Uwe Manthe,Michael Thoss*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文将费米子层级运动方程（HEOM）置于孪生空间表述下，采用多组态含时Hartree（MCTDH）方法高效传播其层级张量，构成HEOM+MCTDH方法，并在Holstein型纳米结及强分子-电极耦合的振动耦合纳米结中验证了其效率与可达规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM是开放量子系统动力学数值精确的强有力方法，但层级方程数目随截断阶数与浴模数急剧增长，传统传播方式限制了可处理体系的规模。已有张量列/张量网络方法虽能扩展适用范围，仍需更高效的传播方案以处理更大、更接近实际、乃至此前无法全量子模拟的强耦合体系。

Method: 基于已有的HEOM孪生空间（twin-space）表述，将层级自由度视为多组态波函数的模式，用MCTDH对费米子HEOM进行传播，从而以较少组态压缩层级张量；随后以Holstein型纳米结的电子输运计算进行基准测试，并应用于分子-电极强耦合的复杂振动耦合纳米结模型。

Result: HEOM+MCTDH在Holstein型纳米结电子输运基准计算中给出可靠结果并体现效率优势；在强分子-电极耦合的振动耦合纳米结模型上完成了此前无法实现的全量子模拟，展示了该方法可扩展至更复杂、更现实的体系。

Conclusion: MCTDH与孪生空间HEOM的结合为费米子开放量子系统提供了一种高效的层级传播手段，可显著拓展HEOM的适用范围至更真实、强耦合的纳米结体系，并为后续张量方法改进提供基准。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) are a powerful, numerically exact approach for simulating the time evolution of open quantum systems. Recently, several tensor-train- and tensor-network-based approaches have been suggested and realized to extend the range of applicability of the HEOM approach to larger and more realistic systems. In this paper, we build upon the existing twin-space formulation of the HEOM and present a novel approach to propagate the fermionic HEOM efficiently using the multi-configurational time-dependent Hartree method (MCTDH). We benchmark the resulting HEOM+MCTDH method with electron transport calculations for a Holstein-type nanojunction model. Furthermore, we demonstrate the capabilities of the HEOM+MCTDH method by applying it to a more complex vibronic coupling nanojunction model with strong molecule-electrode coupling for which fully quantum simulations have not been feasible previously.

</details>


### [124] [Classical control of quantum-state relaxation in confined molecular hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09850)
*Nathan McLane,Richard C. Remsing,Leah G. Dodson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究固态基质环境如何调控H₂的 ortho–para 核自旋转化动力学，发现提高H₂负载量会通过改变声子浴与应变诱导的H₂团簇化而抑制转化，建立了"宏观集体响应—微观量子态动力学"的多尺度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立H₂分子中ortho与para核自旋态间转化是禁阻的，需借助环境实现；但环境如何同时决定新连通态之间的动力学尚不清楚。作者希望厘清宿主环境在"建立连通性"与"调控其转化速率"两方面的作用。

Method: 将H₂限域于晶态分子固体（CO₂、N₂O）中，以转动分辨红外光谱在10 K下直接测定态分辨的转化动力学；结合分子动力学与Monte Carlo模拟分析浓度依赖性来源。

Result: H₂浓度升高使核自旋转化速率降低近一个数量级，20% H₂负载时几乎完全抑制。模拟给出两个互补机制：可介导弛豫的热布居声子模式发生改变；应变驱动的H₂团簇化使分子逐步脱离起转化"开关"作用的宿主晶场。

Conclusion: H₂负载产生的应变重组局域分子环境，从而同时改变声子浴及分子与声子浴的耦合；由此提出凝聚相环境调控有效两能级系统连通性与动力学的多尺度机制。

Abstract: Quantum-state conversion between the ortho and para nuclear-spin states of H$_2$ is forbidden in the isolated molecule but these states can become connected through interactions with a surrounding environment. How that same environment governs the dynamics of the newly connected states remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate this problem using H$_2$ confined in crystalline molecular solids, where the host environment selectively enables conversion between a pair of otherwise disconnected ortho-H$_2$ and para-H$_2$ states. Rotationally resolved infrared spectroscopy directly determines the state-specific conversion dynamics in crystalline CO$_2$ and N$_2$O at 10 K, revealing that increasing H$_2$ concentration suppresses nuclear-spin conversion by nearly an order of magnitude, reaching complete suppression by 20% H$_2$ loading. Molecular dynamics and Monte Carlo simulations identify two complementary origins of this concentration dependence: changes in the thermally populated phonon modes available to mediate relaxation, and strain-driven H$_2$ clustering that progressively removes molecules from the host crystal field responsible for enabling conversion. The resulting picture connects a classical collective response of the host to microscopic quantum-state dynamics: H$_2$ loading generates strain that reorganizes the local molecular environment, thereby modifying both the phonon bath and the molecular coupling to that bath. These results establish a multiscale mechanism by which condensed-phase environments can govern the connectivity and dynamics of an effective two-level system.

</details>

### [125] [Continuum mean-field theory of spatiotemporal pattern formation in a three-state active Potts model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09180)
*Yuta Tateyama,Songhao Yin,Hiroaki Ito,Hiroyuki Kitahata,Hiroshi Noguchi*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 研究含循环转移偏置的三态活性Potts模型的时空动力学，通过空间平均场导出的连续场方程揭示Hopf分岔、复Ginzburg-Landau临界动力学、极限环与双稳、螺旋波，并半解析预测曲率依赖的波前速度与螺旋波角速度/螺距，最后与Monte Carlo模拟对比以评估涨落效应与平均场有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三态活性系统在循环（非平衡）转移偏置下的时空有序行为，特别是螺旋波这类拓扑缺陷如何从微观规则涌现，并量化平均场描述在有限噪声下的适用范围。

Method: 以微观Glauber型动力学为出发点，采用空间平均场近似推导宏观连续场方程；进行线性稳定性与弱非线性分析（导出复Ginzburg-Landau方程）；在一维行波波前微扰理论基础上推导圆形波前曲率-速度关系及螺旋波的旋转角速度与螺距；并与离散Monte Carlo模拟对比。

Result: 随耦合强度增大系统发生Hopf分岔，其附近临界振幅由复Ginzburg-Landau方程刻画；均匀极限下存在极限环振荡与有序态，二者之间为以ℤ₃对称异宿分岔为界的双稳区间；二维中可维持稳定旋转螺旋波；半解析预测的曲率依赖波前速度、螺旋波角速度与螺距与模拟相符。

Conclusion: 循环偏置的三态活性Potts模型可作为一个可解析处理的范例，统一描述从Hopf分岔到螺旋波形成的时空图样；一维行波微扰理论对螺旋波选择性给出了定量预测，Monte Carlo对比明确了热涨落在何种参数区偏离平均场描述。

Abstract: We investigate the spatiotemporal dynamics of a three-state active Potts model with a cyclic transition bias. Starting from microscopic Glauber-type dynamics, we derive macroscopic continuum field equations through a spatial mean-field approximation. Linear stability and weakly nonlinear analyses reveal that increasing the coupling strength induces a Hopf bifurcation, near which the critical amplitude dynamics is described by the complex Ginzburg--Landau equation. In the spatially homogeneous limit, the system exhibits limit-cycle oscillations and ordered states, with an intermediate regime of bistability bounded by a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-symmetric heteroclinic bifurcation. In two dimensions, the system sustains stable rotating spiral waves. Using perturbation theory based on one-dimensional traveling fronts, we semianalytically predict the curvature-dependent propagation velocity of circular fronts, as well as the rotational angular velocity and pitch of spiral waves. Finally, we compare the continuum model with discrete Monte Carlo simulations to clarify the role of thermal fluctuations and evaluate the validity of the mean-field approximation.

</details>

### [126] [A Quantum Mechanical Description of Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09861)
*Ghansham Chandel,Siddhartha Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出以"能量耗散率"和"动量生成率"（而非能量与动量本身）作为描述游动活性物质的基本动力学量，并据此构造其拉格朗日量与哈密顿量，进而在傅里叶空间中得到类量子力学的算符化描述：单个体波函数满足带最小耦合的狄拉克型方程，并给出以非运动基础代谢率为等效势的薛定谔型方程；最后用活性力的多极展开（重点为单极与偶极）形式化游泳体与流体的双向耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命可居环境离不开耗散与熵力，活性物质必须依靠持续的能量与动量通量才能生存与传播，因此其动力学不能简单沿用基本粒子的"能量—动量"描述。作者希望为游泳活性物质建立一套以速率量为核心的动力学框架，并借助活性系统广泛存在的周期性非重复运动（信息编码于周期之中）把傅里叶分析与量子力学算符工具引入活性物质研究，同时统一刻画资源稀缺约束下的种群密度、单个体概率分布以及游泳体与流体的耦合。

Method: (1) 以能量耗散率与动量生成率为基本动力学变量，构造活性物质运动的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量；(2) 引入复场描述单个体演化概率分布及非相互作用活性粒子在资源稀缺约束下的种群密度；(3) 在傅里叶空间做算符化处理，得到与带最小耦合狄拉克方程类似的单个体波函数演化，其中规范对称性对应于控制方程在任意资源景观下的普适性；(4) 给出类薛定谔方程，其等效势项表示环境所要求的非运动基础代谢率；(5) 通过活性力的多极展开（单极、偶极）形式化游泳体与周围流体的双向耦合，每一极对应一种活性运动模式。

Result: 建立了活性物质的速率型拉格朗日/哈密顿表述；导出与最小耦合狄拉克方程类似的单个体波函数方程，并识别出规范对称性作为跨任意资源景观普适性的体现；给出以基础代谢率为等效势的薛定谔型方程；以多极展开（力单极、力偶极）给出游泳体—流体双向耦合的形式化描述，并将各极与不同活性运动模式对应。

Conclusion: 作者主张活性物质的基本动力学量应为能量耗散率与动量生成率，由此可在傅里叶空间将活性游泳体问题映射为类量子（算符化）框架：狄拉克型方程描述单个体波函数与资源约束下的种群，薛定谔型方程中的等效势体现环境强制的基础代谢代价，多极展开则提供游泳体与流体耦合的统一表述。该框架把活性物质动力学、量子力学式形式体系与流体多极耦合联系起来。

Abstract: The natural environments habitable to life are inseparable from dissipative and entropic forces, which mandate active matter to operate on constant fluxes of energy and momentum to survive and propagate through spacetime. In contrast to fundamental particles which are characterized by energy and momentum, we postulate that energy expenditure rate and momentum generation rate are the fundamental dynamical quantities driving this evolution of swimming active matter and accordingly construct the corresponding Lagrangian and Hamiltonian of motion of the active matter. Furthermore, living systems broadly employ periodic, non-repeating motion for both locomotion and various biological functions, with meaningful information stored in the periods of these functions. Consequently, analyzing these dynamics in the Fourier space lands us at the familiar tools of the operator based, quantum mechanics. This is accomplished by introducing a complex field to describe the evolving probability distribution of a single unit, as well as the population density of mutually non-interacting active particles under the constraint of scarce resources. The resulting single-unit wavefunction evolves analogously to the Dirac equation with minimal coupling, wherein gauge symmetry reflects the generality of the governing equations across arbitrary resource landscapes. Additionally, we present a Schrödinger-like equation where the effective potential term represents the environmentally mandated, non-motile base metabolic rate. Finally, we formalize the bidirectional coupling between active swimmers and the surrounding fluid via a multipole expansion of the active force with an emphasis on force monopoles and dipoles, where each pole corresponds to a distinct mode of active motility.

</details>

### [127] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Annealing for Protein Side-Chain Optimization: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08696)
*Valeria B. C. Visani,Simone Reale,Alfonso Gautieri,Elisabetta Di Nitto*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该可行性研究以计算蛋白质设计中的侧链优化问题作为原生二元二次规划（BQP）的工业实例，在20个实验解析蛋白结构衍生的40个算例（最多约200个氨基酸、2000个二元变量）上，比较D-Wave混合求解器（5秒）与商用求解器Gurobi（10秒、1000秒、4小时）的表现。结果显示混合求解器在有认证最优解时均能达到，小规模实例上持平Gurobi，大规模实例上找到更低能量解，证明其在BQP上的竞争力可从合成基准扩展到真实问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典退火求解器正走向工业应用，但其相对经典优化的优势似乎仅限于很窄的问题类别。近期对D-Wave混合求解器与业界领先经典求解器的系统基准测试表明，它仅在二元二次规划上最具竞争力，而极少有工业问题具有这种形式。因此，关键在于寻找可被建模为该结构的真实工业问题，以验证这种竞争力是否真实存在。

Method: 提出将蛋白质侧链优化（计算蛋白质设计的核心步骤）作为候选问题：它天然是二元二次规划，且其应用场景需要成批的低能解，而退火器恰好能自然输出这类解池。开展可行性研究，在40个算例（源自20个实验测定的蛋白结构，规模达约200个氨基酸和2000个二元变量）上，将运行5秒的D-Wave混合求解器与运行10秒、1000秒和4小时的商业求解器Gurobi进行对比。

Result: 混合求解器在存在认证最优解的算例上均达到该最优解；在较小规模算例上与Gurobi表现相当；在较大规模算例上优于Gurobi，找到能量更低的解。

Conclusion: 混合求解器在二元二次规划上的竞争力此前仅在合成基准中被观察到，本研究证明其可扩展到一个真实世界问题。这为进一步与针对蛋白质侧链优化的专用经典启发式算法进行比较提供了动机。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical annealing solvers are moving from research laboratories toward industrial use, but their advantage over classical optimization appears to be confined to a narrow class of problems. A recent systematic benchmark of the D-Wave hybrid solver against industry-leading classical solvers, across several classes of optimization problems, found it to be most competitive on binary quadratic programs, while noting that few industrial problems take this form. Therefore, identifying real-world industrial problems that can be modeled with this structure is a key challenge. We propose protein side-chain optimization, a core step of computational protein design, as one of these problems: it is natively a binary quadratic program, and its application context requires pools of low-energy solutions, which annealers naturally return. In this feasibility study, we test whether the competitiveness of the hybrid solver against industry-leading classical solvers extends to this problem. On 40 instances derived from 20 experimentally determined protein structures with up to about 200 amino acids and 2000 binary variables, we compare the results achieved with a hybrid solver, run for 5 seconds, against those of the commercial solver Gurobi, run for 10 seconds, 1000 seconds, and 4 hours. The hybrid solver reaches the certified optimum whenever one is available, matches Gurobi's performance on smaller instances, and outperforms it on larger instances, finding lower-energy solutions. These results show that the competitiveness of the hybrid solver on binary quadratic programs, so far observed only on a synthetic benchmark, extends to a real-world problem. This motivates comparisons with classical heuristics specific to protein side-chain optimization.

</details>

### [128] [Deep-UV All-dielectric qBIC Metasurface for Refractive Index Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08733)
*Beáta Idesová,Alexander A. Antonov,Lina Rohrer,Leonardo de Souza Menezes,Andreas Tittl,Tomáš Šikola,Filip Ligmajer*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了基于二氧化铪（HfO2）的全介质超表面，在225–340 nm深紫外波段支持准连续域束缚态（qBIC）共振，品质因子达500，并在微流控集成下实现约60 nm/RIU灵敏度与40 RIU⁻¹的品质因数，为无标记深紫外生物传感提供低损耗平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持qBIC的全介质超表面是生物传感的有力平台，但相关工作主要集中于可见与近红外波段。将共振拓展至深紫外（DUV）可直接探测生物分子的本征电子吸收，然而在材料选择与纳米加工方面存在显著挑战。

Method: 采用低损耗的HfO2作为超表面材料，设计并制备支持DUV qBIC的全介质超表面；搭建微流控池与反射模式光学表征系统，通过引入水性缓冲液进行折射率传感测试。

Result: 在225–340 nm波段实现qBIC共振，品质因子最高达500；水性缓冲液的引入进一步提升品质因子；折射率灵敏度约60 nm/RIU，工作品质因数达40 RIU⁻¹。

Conclusion: 该低损耗HfO2超表面成功将qBIC共振拓展至深紫外波段，并具备可实用的折射率传感性能，为无标记DUV生物传感奠定了可靠基础。

Abstract: All-dielectric metasurfaces supporting quasi-bound states in the continuum (qBICs) have emerged as powerful platforms for biosensing, yet their operation remains largely concentrated in the visible and near-infrared regimes. Extending these resonances into the deep-ultraviolet (DUV) to probe the intrinsic electronic absorption of biomolecules presents substantial material and nanofabrication challenges. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a hafnium dioxide (HfO$_{2}$) metasurface supporting DUV qBICs in the wavelength range from 225 nm to 340 nm with quality factors reaching 500. We further demonstrate its refractive index sensing capabilities through integration with a microfluidic cell and optical characterization in reflection mode. We show that the introduction of an aqueous buffer leads to a further quality factor enhancement. Achieving a sensitivity of up to approximately 60 nm/RIU and an operational figure of merit of 40 $\mathrm{RIU}^{-1}$, this low-loss HfO$_{2}$ metasurface provides a robust foundation for label-free DUV biosensing.

</details>


### [129] [Reconstructing intracell polarization with calibrated sapphire-window optics for collimated blue-light generation in a rubidium cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09441)
*Sarah Ashby,David Bostanjyan,Mikayel Khanbekyan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文研究蓝宝石窗口铷蒸汽池中420 nm准直蓝光(CBL)的偏振依赖，通过双波长斯托克斯态标定窗口引入的偏振旋转，并用九系数二阶响应模型拟合2574个CBL强度测量，交叉验证R²为0.636–0.828。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蓝宝石窗口会在泵浦光到达蒸汽前改变其偏振态，使偏振依赖的非线性发射解释复杂化；需要一套可校准、可复现的偏振坐标框架来定量描述四波混频产生的CBL。

Method: 在780/776 nm共振四波混频激发下，于池前外部参考面测量780 nm泵浦斯托克斯态以标定HWP/QWP制备光路；在90/100/110 °C、两种传播取向下记录266个池后外部输出斯托克斯态，进行配对双波长测量，得到相对斯托克斯空间映射；基于校准后的共同制备泵浦描述，对2574个420 nm滤波CBL强度测量用含九个系数的二阶响应模型做交叉验证拟合。

Result: 池前780/776平均分离为4.29°，整池旋转随温度与取向为22.20°–32.02°，rms残差4.83°–12.87°；六组温度–取向数据集的交叉验证R²为0.636–0.828。

Conclusion: 配对双波长测量为每组数据集确定了固定的相对斯托克斯空间映射，其残差即相关双泵浦约化的实验不确定度；该框架将重建的蒸汽入口偏振坐标与整池双波长标定结合，为蓝宝石窗口蒸汽池中的偏振控制非线性光学实验提供实用基础。

Abstract: We study the polarization dependence of collimated blue light (CBL) generated near 420~nm by resonant four-wave mixing in thermal rubidium vapor in a sapphire-window cell driven at 780 and 776~nm. Sapphire windows can transform the polarization of pump light before it reaches the vapor, complicating the interpretation of polarization-dependent nonlinear emission. We measured the 780-nm pump Stokes state at an external before-cell reference plane to calibrate the common HWP/QWP preparation optics and recorded 266 off-resonant external output Stokes states after the complete sapphire-window rubidium cell at 90, 100, and 110~$^\circ$C in two propagation orientations. Direct paired-wavelength measurements show a mean before-cell 780/776 separation of $4.29^\circ$ and temperature- and orientation-specific complete-cell rotations of $22.20^\circ$--$32.02^\circ$, with rms residuals of $4.83^\circ$--$12.87^\circ$. Using this calibrated co-prepared pump description, we analyzed 2574 measurements of total spectrally filtered 420-nm CBL intensity. Across the six temperature--orientation data sets, a complete nine-coefficient second-order response yielded cross-validated $R^2$ values of 0.636 to 0.828. These paired-wavelength measurements establish, for each temperature--orientation data set, a fixed relative Stokes-space map between the two co-prepared pump trajectories, with the residual of this mapping defining the experimental uncertainty of the correlated two-pump reduction used in the response model. Thus, the resulting framework combines a reconstructed vapor-entrance polarization coordinate with direct two-wavelength calibration of the assembled cell and provides a practical basis for polarization-controlled nonlinear-optical experiments in sapphire-window vapor cells.

</details>
